Method: MOTAB包含三个关键步骤：1) 动态监控学生模型的在线生成，设定自适应安全边界；2) 当学生生成偏离并超过安全阈值时，回退到最后一个安全状态；3) 利用教师模型介入纠正轨迹。该方法容忍小幅学生错误以缓解暴露偏差，同时阻止次优上下文以避免反向暴露偏差。

Result: 在LIMO-v2和AceReason数据集上的大量实验表明，MOTAB有效缓解了双重暴露偏差，在推理任务上取得了约3%的平均性能提升。

Conclusion: MOTAB通过动态监控和回溯机制，在容忍小错误和阻止次优上下文之间取得平衡，有效解决了LLM推理蒸馏中的双重暴露偏差问题，为长链推理的蒸馏部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in complex reasoning tasks via long chain-of-thought (CoT), yet their immense computational overhead hinders real-world deployment. LLM reasoning distillation addresses this by transferring reasoning capabilities from formidable teacher models to compact student models. However, existing distillation paradigms face a fundamental dilemma. Typical off-policy distillation strictly utilizes teacher-generated golden trajectories, suffering from an exposure bias due to the mismatch between training distributions and student-generated inference contexts, which leads to error cascades in long CoT reasoning. To address this, on-policy distillation allows students to explore their own trajectories, but we demonstrate that it inherently introduces a reciprocal reversed exposure bias: the teacher model also struggles to provide positive guidance when conditioned on student-generated sub-optimal contexts. To resolve this dual exposure biases problem, we propose Monitoring Trajectories and Backtracking when it strays (MOTAB), a new LLM reasoning distillation pipeline. Specifically, MOTAB dynamically monitors the student's on-policy generation against an adaptive safety boundary. When the generation strays and exceeds this threshold, MOTAB backtracks to the last safe state and leverages teacher intervention to correct the course. This approach inherently tolerates minor student errors to mitigate exposure bias, while preventing sub-optimal contexts to circumvent reversed exposure bias. Extensive experiments on the LIMO-v2 and AceReason datasets demonstrate that MOTAB effectively alleviates the dual exposure biases, yielding a roughly 3% average performance improvement in reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [109] [Drifting Objectives for Refining Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19470)
*Daisuke Oba,Hiroki Furuta,Naoaki Okazaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokenDrift方法，将反非对称不动点漂移目标从连续生成器迁移到离散扩散语言模型(DDLM)中。通过将分类预测提升为软token特征，在冻结语义空间中应用反非对称漂移，并将停止梯度特征目标回传到DDLM的logits，解决了离散文本不可微的核心挑战。实验表明，在掩码和均匀状态扩散骨干上，TokenDrift在固定NFE生成质量上优于匹配的继续训练基线，在MDLM和DUO上分别将4 NFE的Gen.-PPL降低了89%和86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续生成器中的漂移方法表明，部分采样时校正可被吸收到训练中，但将其迁移到DDLM面临离散文本非可微、分类预测不提供连续样本等核心挑战。作者希望克服这些障碍，将漂移原理应用于离散扩散语言模型。

Method: 提出TokenDrift方法：1）将DDLM的分类预测提升为软token特征；2）在冻结语义空间中应用反非对称漂移；3）将停止梯度特征目标通过反向传播回传到DDLM的logits。该方法在掩码和均匀状态扩散骨干上进行受控的继续训练实验。

Result: TokenDrift显著提升了固定NFE下的生成质量：在4 NFEs时，MDLM的Gen.-PPL降低89%，DUO降低86%。

Conclusion: 漂移目标可以为DDLM提供实用的精炼目标，有效提升离散扩散模型的生成质量。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (DDLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising categorical token sequences, while recent drifting methods for continuous generators suggest that part of this sampling-time correction can instead be absorbed into training through an anti-symmetric fixed-point objective. We study how to transfer this principle to DDLMs, where the main challenge is the interface with discrete text: hard token samples are non-differentiable, and categorical predictions do not directly provide continuous samples to drift. We formulate TokenDrift, a drifting objective that lifts categorical predictions to soft-token features, applies anti-symmetric drifting in a frozen semantic space, and backpropagates the resulting stop-gradient feature target to DDLM logits. In controlled continual-training experiments with masked and uniform-state diffusion backbones, TokenDrift improves fixed-NFE generation quality over matched continuation baselines, reducing Gen.-PPL at 4 NFEs by 89% on MDLM and 86% on DUO. These results suggest that drifting can provide a practical refinement objective for DDLMs.

</details>


### [110] [Base Models Look Human To AI Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19516)
*Yixuan Even Xu,Ziqian Zhong,Aditi Raghunathan,Fei Fang,J. Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现商业AI文本检测器（GPTZero、Pangram）对基础模型生成的文本常误判为人类，但对指令微调模型生成的文本则能有效识别。基于此，提出HIP（迭代释义人性化）方法——对基础模型进行最小微调得到释义器并迭代应用，在语义保留和检测规避之间取得更优权衡。实验表明当前检测器更多追踪的是指令微调和局部上下文的伪影，而非机器文本的不变特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示商业AI文本检测器在基础模型与指令微调模型上的不一致表现，指出当前检测器实际追踪的是指令微调和局部上下文伪影而非机器文本的本质特征，并基于此提出更有效的规避方法。

Method: HIP（Humanization by Iterative Paraphrasing）：对基础模型进行最小微调得到释义器，然后迭代应用该释义器对文本进行改写，以在保持语义的同时提高检测器的人类似性。

Result: 在Llama-3和Qwen-3系列（0.6B-70B）上，HIP在语义保留与检测规避之间取得更优权衡；GPTZero和Pangram对基础模型生成文本倾向于判为人类，但对指令微调模型生成文本则能识别。

Conclusion: 当前检测器更多捕捉的是指令微调和局部上下文的伪影而非机器生成文本的固有特征，未来检测器设计需要更明确地建模这些因素。

Abstract: As AI-generated text enters the real-world at scale, institutions increasingly use commercial AI-text detectors, especially in education and academic-integrity workflows. We report a surprising empirical finding about such systems: when evaluated by GPTZero and Pangram, generated text from base models is often judged overwhelmingly human, whereas text generated by their instruction-tuned counterparts is not. Building on this observation, we propose Humanization by Iterative Paraphrasing (HIP), a detector-agnostic pipeline that minimally fine-tunes a base model into a paraphraser and applies it iteratively. Compared with the baselines we test, HIP yields a stronger trade-off between semantic preservation and detector evasion on commercial detectors. Across Llama-3 and Qwen-3 families, spanning model sizes from 0.6B to 70B, HIP consistently improves detector human-likeness. Our findings suggest that current detectors are tracking artifacts of instruction tuning and local context more than any invariant notion of machine-generated text. This, in turn, calls for detector designs that model these factors more explicitly.

</details>


### [111] [Investigating Cross-Modal Skill Injection: Scenarios, Methods, and Hyperparameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19523)
*Zhiyu Xu,Lean Wang,Yuanxin Liu,Lei Li,Hao Zhou,Fandong Meng,Jie Zhou,Xu Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了跨模态技能注入（将领域专家LLM的能力注入VLM）在场景、方法和超参数三个维度上的表现，发现其在指令遵循和跨语言场景中表现良好，但在数学推理上效果不佳；TA和DARE等经典方法优于其他合并方法，且超参数调优至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在通用多模态理解上表现出色，但难以高效获取持续演进的领域特定技能。传统SFT需要大量数据标注和计算资源，而模型合并提供了一种高效替代方案。然而，现有研究缺乏对跨模态技能注入的适用性和方法的系统分析。

Method: 从三个维度系统研究跨模态技能注入：场景（指令遵循、跨语言、数学推理）、方法（TA、DARE等经典合并方法）和超参数分析。将领域专家LLM注入VLM，评估不同设置下的性能表现。

Result: 跨模态技能注入在指令遵循和跨语言场景中表现良好，但在数学推理场景中效果不佳；TA和DARE等经典合并方法优于其他替代方法；超参数调优对这些经典方法的性能有关键影响。

Conclusion: 跨模态技能注入是一种高效的VLM能力增强范式，但在不同场景下效果存在显著差异，需要根据具体任务选择合适的合并方法和超参数配置。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in general multi-modal understanding; yet they struggle to efficiently acquire continually evolving domain-specific skills. Conventional approaches to enhancing VLM capabilities, such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), require extensive dataset curation and substantial computational resources. Model merging has emerged as an efficient alternative that enables the transfer of domain-specific expertise from Large Language Models (LLMs) to VLMs without incurring additional training data requirements or significant computational overhead. Unlike conventional merging of homogeneous LLMs, which mainly aggregates existing capabilities, cross-modal skill injection aims to induce emergent cross-modal capabilities by integrating a domain-expert LLM into a VLM. However, existing research lacks a systematic analysis of the applicability and methodology of cross-modal skill injection. In this study, we investigate cross-modal skill injection across three main aspects: scenarios, methods, and hyperparameters. For scenarios, we find that cross-modal skill injection generally performs well in instruction-following and cross-lingual settings, yet struggles with mathematical reasoning. For methods, we find that classic approaches such as TA and DARE consistently achieve superior performance over alternative merging methods. We also provide a systematic and quantitative analysis of the hyperparameter tuning that these classic methods critically depend on.

</details>


### [112] [A Data-Driven Approach to Idiomaticity Based on Experts' Criteria in Theoretical Linguistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19575)
*Elena Mikhalkova,Anastasiya Vishnyakova,Anastasiya Drozdova,Polina Gavin,Aleksander Zhmykhov,Timofey Protasov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基于16项词汇、语法及其他标准，对286个多词表达（MWE）进行了数据分析。专家标注结果显示，不存在绝对习语化的表达。词汇标准影响最大，语法标准受特定条件约束，古旧词汇和语法影响MWE被单个词替换的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多词表达（MWE）的习语性程度，检验理论上提出的各项标准在实际数据中的分布情况，并考察是否存在绝对习语化的表达。

Method: 从理论文献中收集286个MWE，由语言学专家根据16项词汇、语法及其他标准进行标注，随后分析这些类别的分布。

Result: 不存在绝对习语化的表达；词汇标准最具影响力；语法标准仅在特定条件下起作用；古旧词汇和语法结构影响MWE能否被单个词替换。

Conclusion: 习语性是一个程度问题，而非二分特征。词汇标准是决定习语性的主要因素，语法标准和古旧成分则起到条件性调节作用。

Abstract: The article observes data analysis of 286 multi-word expressions (MWEs) based on 16 lexical, grammatical and other criteria described in theoretical books and papers on the notion of idiomaticity. MWEs were collected from the same theoretical sources, and a set of experts in linguistics annotated them with these categories. The distribution of categories shows that there are no absolutely idiomatic expressions. Lexical criteria seem to be the most influential; grammatical criteria are bound to certain conditions; presence of obsolete words and grammar influence ability of an MWE to be replaced with one word.

</details>


### [113] [GoLongRL: Capability-Oriented Long Context Reinforcement Learning with Multitask Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19577)
*Minxuan Lv,Tiehua Mei,Tanlong Du,Junmin Chen,Zhenpeng Su,Ziyang Chen,Ziqi Wang,Zhennan Wu,Ruotong Pan,jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GoLongRL 提出了一种面向长上下文的强化学习训练方案，包含能力导向的数据构建（23K RLVR样本，覆盖9种任务类型）和 TMN-Reweight 多任务优化方法，在开源模型上取得了与 DeepSeek-R1-0528 和 Qwen3-235B 相当的长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文 RL 方法的数据构建过度依赖设计复杂的检索路径，导致任务覆盖同质化且奖励设计不能反映实际长上下文需求。

Method: (1) 能力导向数据构建：基于长上下文能力分类体系，构建涵盖9种任务类型、23K样本的数据集，包含精选开源样本和基于真实文档合成的 QA 对。(2) TMN-Reweight：结合任务级均值归一化（对齐跨任务奖励尺度）和难度自适应加权（改进优势估计），用于异构多任务优化。

Result: 在相同 GRPO 设置下，该数据集优于闭源 QwenLong-L1.5 数据集；Qwen3-30B-A3B 模型长上下文性能与 DeepSeek-R1-0528 和 Qwen3-235B-A22B 相当；TMN-Reweight 进一步提升了平均性能，且通用能力保持或改善。

Conclusion: 更广泛的任务覆盖和更大的奖励多样性显著有利于长上下文能力提升；TMN-Reweight 有效解决了异构奖励带来的优化挑战。

Abstract: We present GoLongRL, a fully open-source, capability-oriented post-training recipe for long-context reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Existing long-context RL methods often treat data construction as a matter of designing increasingly complex retrieval paths, leading to homogeneous task coverage and reward formulations that inadequately reflect practical long-context requirements. Our work offers two contributions. (1) Capability-oriented data construction with full open release. We openly release a dataset of 23K RLVR samples, the complete construction pipeline, and all training code. Guided by a taxonomy of long-context capabilities, the dataset spans 9 task types, each paired with its natural evaluation metric. It comprises curated open-source samples from established corpora and synthetic samples whose QA pairs are generated from real source documents such as books, academic papers, and multi-turn dialogues. Under the same vanilla GRPO setup, our dataset alone outperforms the closed-source QwenLong-L1.5 dataset. Moreover, our Qwen3-30B-A3B model trained on this data delivers long-context performance comparable to DeepSeek-R1-0528 and Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507, suggesting that broader coverage and greater reward diversity substantially benefit long-context capability improvement. (2) TMN-Reweight for heterogeneous multitask optimization. To address optimization challenges from heterogeneous rewards, we propose TMN-Reweight, which combines task-level mean normalization for cross-task reward scale alignment with difficulty-adaptive weighting for more reliable advantage estimation. TMN-Reweight further improves average performance over vanilla GRPO, with general capabilities preserved or improved across reported evaluations.

</details>


### [114] [LLMEval-Logic: A Solver-Verified Chinese Benchmark for Logical Reasoning of LLMs with Adversarial Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19597)
*Ming Zhang,Qiyuan Peng,Yinxi Wei,Yujiong Shen,Kexin Tan,Yuhui Wang,Zhenghao Xiang,Junjie Ye,Zhangyue Yin,Zhiheng Xi,Shihan Dou,Tao Gui,Maxm Pan,Ruizhi Yang,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMEval-Logic是一个中文逻辑推理基准，包含246题的Base子集（含1,400个专家评分原子）和190题的Hard子集（含938个多步子问题）。评估14个前沿LLM显示最佳模型Hard准确率仅37.5%，最高形式化得分仅60.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑推理基准多通过模板从采样公式生成自然语言项，注释粗糙且很快被前沿推理模型饱和，缺乏高质量、可审计的逻辑推理评测资源。

Method: 采用正向编写与专家审计相结合的流水线生成自然语言项及其参考形式化表示，用Z3验证标注答案，构建自然到形式化评分的专家评分标准，通过闭环对抗工作流强化所选题目。

Result: 评估14个前沿LLM，最佳模型Hard子集准确率仅37.5%，使用参考符号时联合Z3+Rubric形式化最高得分60.16%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在复杂逻辑推理上仍存在显著差距，LLMEval-Logic提供了更具挑战性和可审计性的中文逻辑推理评测基准。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) on natural-language logical reasoning is essential because rule-governed tasks require conclusions to follow strictly from stated premises. Many existing logical-reasoning benchmarks are generated by templating natural-language items from sampled formulas, provide only coarse or unaudited formal annotations, and are now quickly saturated by frontier reasoning models. We present LLMEval-Logic, a Chinese logical reasoning benchmark built from realistic situational scenarios. Its pipeline forward-authors and expert-audits natural-language items together with their reference formalizations, verifies annotated answers with Z3, constructs expert rubrics for natural-to-formal grading, and hardens selected items through a closed-loop adversarial workflow. The benchmark is released in two paired subsets: a 246-item Base subset shipped with 1,400 expert-developed rubric atoms, and a 190-item Hard subset with 938 multi-step sub-questions over closed model spaces. Evaluating 14 frontier LLMs on LLMEval-Logic reveals substantial gaps in current models: the best model reaches only 37.5% Hard Item Accuracy, and even with reference symbols the highest joint Z3+Rubric formalization score among evaluated models reaches only 60.16%. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/llmeval/LLMEval-Logic.

</details>


### [115] [optimize_anything: A Universal API for Optimizing any Text Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19633)
*Lakshya A Agrawal,Donghyun Lee,Shangyin Tan,Wenjie Ma,Karim Elmaaroufi,Rohit Sandadi,Sanjit A. Seshia,Koushik Sen,Dan Klein,Ion Stoica,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Omar Khattab,Alexandros G. Dimakis,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的LLM优化系统，在6个完全不同领域（ARC-AGI推理、云计算调度、CUDA内核生成、圆填充等）均达到SOTA，证明基于LLM的文本优化是一种通用问题求解范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证单一LLM优化系统能否在各种完全不同领域匹敌专业化工具，探索通用问题求解范式的可能性。

Method: 将优化问题统一建模为：改进文本工件（text artifact）+评分函数评估；系统支持单任务搜索、多任务搜索（跨问题迁移）以及对未见输入的泛化；通过消融实验验证可操作侧边信息（actionable side information）和多任务搜索的有效性。

Result: ARC-AGI准确率从32.5%提升至89.5%（近3倍）；云计算成本降低40%；87%生成CUDA内核匹敌或超越PyTorch；圆填充（n=26）超越AlphaEvolve。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的文本搜索是一种通用问题求解范式，能统一传统上需要专用算法的各类领域，且可操作侧边信息和多任务迁移显著提升性能。

Abstract: Can a single LLM-based optimization system match specialized tools across fundamentally different domains? We show that when optimization problems are formulated as improving a text artifact evaluated by a scoring function, a single AI-based optimization system-supporting single-task search, multi-task search with cross-problem transfer, and generalization to unseen inputs-achieves state-of-the-art results across six diverse tasks. Our system discovers agent architectures that nearly triple Gemini Flash's ARC-AGI accuracy (32.5% to 89.5%), finds scheduling algorithms that cut cloud costs by 40%, generates CUDA kernels where 87% match or beat PyTorch, and outperforms AlphaEvolve's reported circle packing solution (n=26). Ablations across three domains reveal that actionable side information yields faster convergence and substantially higher final scores than score-only feedback, and that multi-task search outperforms independent optimization given equivalent per-problem budget through cross-task transfer, with benefits scaling with the number of related tasks. Together, we show for the first time that text optimization with LLM-based search is a general-purpose problem-solving paradigm, unifying tasks traditionally requiring domain-specific algorithms under a single framework. We open-source optimize\_anything with support for multiple backends as part of the GEPA project at https://github.com/gepa-ai/gepa .

</details>


### [116] [K-Quantization and its Impact on Output Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19645)
*Robin Baki Davidsson,Pierre Nugues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了8种大语言模型在2至6比特量化下的性能表现，发现高精度（如8-bit Q8_0）通常带来更好性能但收益递减，激进量化（如2-bit Q2_K）多数情况下仍能保持可接受的准确率，但不同模型和任务间存在差异；7-9B参数的中等模型在效率和资源消耗间达到最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数量巨大导致部署困难，需要高效的模型压缩技术。量化作为一种主流压缩方法，其对LLM性能和准确率的具体影响（尤其是2至6比特范围）尚未被充分研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 选取8种不同规模的LLM，分别在多种量化级别（2-bit至8-bit）下进行测试，评估任务涵盖MMLU-Pro（知识推理）、CRUXEval（代码理解）和MuSR（阅读理解），通过对比不同精度下的性能差异分析量化影响。

Result: 高精度量化（8-bit Q8_0）性能最优但收益递减；2-bit激进量化虽整体可接受但部分模型性能显著下降；大模型对激进量化韧性更强但低精度下仍可能大幅衰退；7-9B参数的中等模型在效率与资源间达到最优平衡。

Conclusion: 低比特量化通常会降低模型性能，但影响程度因模型和任务而异；模型规模与量化精度之间存在权衡关系，中等规模模型（7-9B）在实际部署中更具性价比优势。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have shown their remarkable capacities in many NLP tasks. However, their substantial size often presents challenges for deployment. This necessitates efficient techniques for model compression, with quantization emerging as a prominent solution. Despite its benefits, the exact impact of quantization (from 2- to 6-bit) on the performance and accuracy of LLMs remains an active area of research. This paper investigates the performance of eight LLMs at various quantization levels, focusing on tasks such as MMLU-Pro for knowledge processing and reasoning, CRUXEval for code comprehension, and MuSR for reading comprehension. Our results show a consistent trend where higher precision (e.g., 8-bit Q8\_0) yields improved performance, albeit with diminishing returns. Aggressive quantization (e.g., 2-bit Q2\_K) usually retains acceptable accuracy, though some models show a substantial loss in performance. Our findings indicate that while lower bit precision generally reduces performance, the impact varies across models and tasks. Larger models show greater resilience to aggressive quantization, but can still undergo significant drops at lower precision levels. Mid-sized models in the 7-9 billion parameter range strike an optimal balance between efficiency and resource usage. Such results provide insights into the trade-offs between model size, quantization, and performance.

</details>


### [117] [Can Large Language Models Reliably Correct Errors in Low-Resource ASR? A Contamination-Aware Case Study on West Frisian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19711)
*Yun Hao,Reihaneh Amooie,Wietse de Vries,Rik van Noord,Martijn Wieling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于大语言模型（LLM）的生成式纠错（GER）在低资源语言弗里斯兰语自动语音识别（ASR）中的应用，并通过构建非公开离线数据集控制数据污染，结果表明GER能有效提升ASR性能，最佳结果甚至超过oracle词错误率，且改进反映了真实的纠错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的ASR性能仍然有限，LLM在GER方面虽有前景但尚不清楚其在低资源场景下的有效性，同时数据污染对LLM-based GER改进的影响也未被充分探究。

Method: 使用公共语料库并额外构建弗里斯兰语非公开离线数据集进行评估以控制数据污染；采用LLM-based GER方法（GPT-5.1）进行生成式纠错；进行详细的错误分析以揭示模型纠正模式。

Result: GER在多数设置下提升了ASR性能；最佳GPT-5.1结果超越了oracle词错误率；离线数据集的增益与公开数据集相当，表明改进反映了真实纠错能力。

Conclusion: LLM-based GER能够有效提升低资源语言ASR性能，且数据污染并非主要驱动因素；未来可进一步探索低资源场景下的纠错模式与模型优化。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has improved substantially in recent years, yet performance remains limited for low-resource languages. Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for improving ASR through generative error correction (GER), but their effectiveness in low-resource settings remains underexplored. In addition, it remains unclear to what extent data contamination influences the reported improvements in LLM-based GER. This study investigates LLM-based GER for low-resource Frisian. In addition to a public corpus, we construct and use a Frisian offline dataset with non-public texts for evaluation to control for potential data contamination. Results show that GER improves ASR performance in most settings, with the best GPT-5.1 results surpassing oracle WERs. Comparable gains on the offline dataset indicate that improvements reflect true correction ability. We further provide a detailed error analysis revealing model correction patterns.

</details>


### [118] [LLM-Based Financial Sentiment Analysis in Arabic: Evidence from Saudi Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19714)
*Mona H. Albaqawi,Eman M. Albalkhi,Joud A. Albaiti,Enrico Lopedoto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向沙特市场的阿拉伯语金融情感分析框架，构建了包含84K样本的大型阿拉伯语金融语料库，通过Transformer NER与公司词典结合实现实体链接，采用五类情感标注方案，并在沙特交易所数据上验证了其可靠性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资者情绪影响金融市场，但由于阿拉伯语的复杂语言特性和有限资源，在阿拉伯金融环境中建模情绪仍然面临挑战。

Method: 构建多阶段流水线，包括数据收集、清洗、去重、实体链接和情感标注；使用基于Transformer的命名实体识别（NER）结合 curated 公司词典将文本提及映射到标准化公司标识符；采用五类情感标注方案。

Result: 构建了包含84K样本的阿拉伯语金融数据集，支持公司级别的情感聚合及情感动态与沙特交易所股票市场行为的关联分析，实验结果表明该方法可靠且可扩展。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效整合官方金融新闻和社交媒体信息，捕捉机构与公众投资者情绪，为阿拉伯金融领域的情绪分析提供可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Investor sentiment shapes financial markets, yet modeling sentiment in Arabic financial contexts remains challenging due to linguistic complexity and limited resources. We present an Arabic NLP framework for large-scale financial sentiment analysis tailored to the Saudi market, integrating official financial news and social media to capture institutional and public investor sentiment. The framework constructs a large Arabic financial corpus through a multi-stage pipeline encompassing data collection, cleaning, deduplication, entity linking, and sentiment annotation. Transformer-based NER combined with a curated company lexicon links textual mentions to canonical company identifiers, with sentiment labels assigned using a five-class scheme. The resulting dataset of 84K samples supports company-level sentiment aggregation and analysis of sentiment dynamics relative to stock market behavior on the Saudi Exchange. Experimental results demonstrate reliable and scalable Arabic financial sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [119] [CAIT: A Syntactic Parsing Toolkit for Child-Adult InTeractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19718)
*Francesca Padovani,Xiulin Yang,Bastian Bunzeck,Jaap Jumelet,Yevgen Matusevych,Nathan Schneider,Arianna Bisazza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对CHILDES语料库，本文训练了专门优化的依存句法分析器，并发布了包含词性标注器和构建标签器的开源工具包CAIT，在儿童-成人互动场景下显著优于SpaCy和Stanza等通用解析器，支持大规模、可复现的语言习得研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHILDES是语言习得研究的核心资源，但缺乏专门针对其句法结构分析的计算工具，现有通用解析器在儿童-成人互动场景下表现不佳。

Method: 利用最新发布的UD-English-CHILDES树库（含黄金标准UD标注），训练专门针对CHILDES的依存句法分析器，同时发布词性标注器和话语层面构建标签器，组成CAIT工具包。

Result: CAIT解析器在儿童-成人互动句法模式捕捉上显著优于SpaCy和Stanza等通用英文解析器。

Conclusion: 通过详细的错误分析和追踪CHILDES中句法构建随发展时间分布的案例研究，证明了该工具包在大规模、可复现的语言习得研究中的实用性。

Abstract: CHILDES is a paramount resource for language acquisition studies -- yet computational tools for analyzing its syntactic structure remain limited. Leveraging the recent release of the UD-English-CHILDES treebank with gold-standard Universal Dependencies (UD) annotations, we train a state-of-the-art dependency parser specifically tailored to CHILDES. The parser more accurately captures syntactic patterns in child--adult interactions, outperforming widely used off-the-shelf English parsers, including SpaCy and Stanza. Alongside the parser, we also release a Part-of-Speech tagger and an utterance-level construction tagger, which together form the open-source Syntactic Parsing Toolkit for Child--Adult InTeractions (CAIT). Through a detailed error analysis and a case study tracking the distribution of syntactic constructions across developmental time in CHILDES, we demonstrate the practical utility of the toolkit for large-scale, reproducible research on language acquisition.

</details>


### [120] [Mathematical Reasoning in Large Language Models: Benchmarks, Architectures, Evaluation, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19723)
*Husnain Amjad,Raja Khurram Shahzad,Aamir Shahzad,Mehwish Fatima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了大型语言模型在数学推理领域的最新进展，涵盖约120篇论文，提出了统一的分析框架，包括数据集分类、推理架构与训练策略分析、评估方法比较，并识别了推理忠实性、基准偏差等关键失败模式及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理是教育、科学和工业领域问题解决的核心能力，也是评估AI系统的重要基准。随着LLM推理能力的提升，系统性地理解其在数学推理上的表现变得日益重要。

Method: 通过系统性综述约120篇同行评审论文及预印本，采用结构化分析方法对数据集、架构、训练策略和评估协议进行研究，提出了统一的数学数据集分类体系（预训练语料、监督微调资源、评估基准），并对推理架构与训练策略（工具集成、验证器引导推理、参数高效适配）进行了系统分析。

Result: 建立了统一的数学数据集分类体系和比较评估框架，揭示了最终答案准确率与过程级推理验证之间的差距，识别了推理忠实性、基准偏差和泛化能力不足等反复出现的失败模式。

Conclusion: 未来研究应关注改进符号基础、提升评估可靠性，以及开发更鲁棒和可信的基于LLM的推理系统。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning is essential for problem-solving in education, science, and industry, serving as a crucial benchmark for evaluating artificial intelligence systems. As Large Language Models (LLMs) improve their reasoning capabilities, understanding how well they perform mathematical reasoning has become increasingly important. This survey synthesizes recent advancements in mathematical reasoning with LLMs through a structured analysis of datasets, architectures, training strategies, and evaluation protocols. Our systematic review encompasses approximately 120 peer-reviewed studies and preprints, examining the evolution of this research area and providing a unified analytical framework to understand current progress and limitations. Our study particularly introduces a unified taxonomy of mathematical datasets, distinguishing between pretraining corpora, supervised fine-tuning resources, and evaluation benchmarks across varying levels of reasoning complexity. A systematic analysis of reasoning architectures and training strategies, including tool integration, verifier-guided reasoning, and parameter-efficient adaptation, is presented to assess their effects on reasoning robustness and generalization. Moreover, a comparative evaluation of existing metrics highlights the gap between final-answer accuracy and process-level reasoning verification. By synthesizing insights across these areas, our analysis identifies recurring failure modes, such as reasoning faithfulness issues, benchmark biases, and generalization limitations, and outlines key research directions toward improving symbolic grounding, evaluation reliability, and the development of more robust and trustworthy LLM-based reasoning systems.

</details>


### [121] [ContextRAG: Extraction-Free Hierarchical Graph Construction for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19735)
*Roman Prosvirnin,Sergei Kuznetsov,Seungmin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextRAG是一种图RAG系统，无需LLM进行实体/关系抽取，而是通过残差量化k-means和形式概念分析构建模糊概念图，在130个任务的测试中仅使用30次LLM调用和22,073个token建立索引，相比HiRAG需要超过2300万token，ContextRAG在F1上达到33.6%，多跳任务F1达36.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图RAG系统在索引阶段依赖LLM抽取实体、关系和摘要，导致token成本和墙钟时间随语料规模线性增长，效率低下。

Method: 采用残差量化k-means对块嵌入进行聚类，结合Lukasiewicz剩余逻辑的形式概念分析构建模糊概念图；通过软模糊连接和交运算推导类桥和类交的上下文节点，而非由LLM生成图边。

Result: 在130个任务的UltraDomain子集上仅用30次LLM调用和22,073个token建立索引；整体F1为33.6%，多跳任务F1为36.8%。至少检索到一个格推导节点的查询F1高出3.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 无需LLM进行实体/关系抽取即可构建有效的图RAG系统，大幅降低索引成本，且在多跳推理任务上表现良好。

Abstract: Graph-structured retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can improve answer quality on multi-hop questions, but many current systems rely on large language models (LLMs) to extract entities, relations, and summaries during indexing. These calls add token and wall-clock costs that grow with corpus size. We present ContextRAG, a graph RAG system whose graph topology is constructed without LLM-based entity or relation extraction. ContextRAG derives a fuzzy concept graph over chunk embeddings using residual-quantization k-means and Formal Concept Analysis with Lukasiewicz residuated logic. Bridge-like and meet-derived context nodes are induced by soft fuzzy join and meet operations, rather than by LLM-written graph edges. On a 130-task UltraDomain subset, ContextRAG builds its index with 30 LLM calls and 22,073 tokens. In contrast, a local HiRAG reproduction stress test required 870 indexing calls and 3.54M tokens on a 20-task subset before failing during graph construction; linear extrapolation to 130 tasks implies over 23M indexing tokens. ContextRAG obtains 33.6% F1 overall and 36.8% F1 on multi-hop tasks. An activation analysis shows that queries retrieving at least one lattice-derived node in the top five achieve +3.9 percentage points F1 over queries that do not; this association is diagnostic rather than causal.

</details>


### [122] [TERGAD: Structure-Aware Text-Enhanced Representations for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19738)
*Wen Shi,Zhe Wang,Huafei Huang,Qing Qing,Ziqi Xu,Qixin Zhang,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TERGAD提出了一种利用大语言模型将节点拓扑属性转化为自然语言描述，从而增强结构语义的图异常检测数据增强框架，通过门控双分支自编码器融合原始属性与LLM语义嵌入，基于重构误差进行异常检测，在六个数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的图异常检测方法在表示学习过程中通常利用原始文本特征整合结构上下文，但往往忽略了节点的结构上下文信息，导致难以检测由节点自身内容与其拓扑角色不一致所引发的复杂异常。

Method: 首先将节点级拓扑属性转化为描述性自然语言叙述；利用大语言模型处理这些叙述以获取高层语义嵌入；通过门控双分支自编码器将语义嵌入与原始节点属性进行自适应融合，联合重构图结构和节点特征；基于综合重构误差计算异常分数。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集上，TERGAD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines（始终超越最先进基线方法），消融实验验证了结构语义引导的不可或缺性以及门控融合机制的有效性。

Conclusion: TERGAD通过LLM驱动的结构语义增强有效弥补了现有方法忽略节点结构上下文的缺陷，为图异常检测提供了一种新颖的数据增强范式。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to identify atypical graph entities, such as nodes, edges, or substructures, that deviate significantly from the majority. While existing text-rich approaches typically integrate structural context into the data representation pipeline using raw textual features, they often neglect the structural context of nodes. This limitation hinders their ability to detect sophisticated anomalies arising from inconsistencies between a node's inherent content and its topological role. To bridge this gap, we propose TERGAD (Structure-aware Text-enhanced Representations for Graph Anomaly Detection), A novel data augmentation framework that enriches structural semantics for GAD via the semantic reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Specifically, TERGAD translates node-level topological properties into descriptive natural language narratives, which are subsequently processed by an LLM to derive high-level semantic embeddings. These embeddings are then adaptively fused with original node attributes through a gated dual-branch autoencoder to jointly reconstruct both graph structure and node features. The anomaly score is computed based on the integrated reconstruction error, effectively capturing deviations in both observable attributes and LLM-informed semantic expectations. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that TERGAD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, our ablation studies validate the indispensable role of structural semantic guidance and the efficacy of the gated fusion mechanism. Code is available at https://github.com/Kantorakitty/TERGAD-main.

</details>


### [123] [Synthesis and Evaluation of Long-term History-aware Medical Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19766)
*Hebin Hu,Renke Dai,Ah-Hwee Tan,Yilin Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MediLongChat，一个通过知识引导的三阶段合成框架生成的长期医疗对话数据集，并建立了三种推理基准任务来评估医疗智能体的记忆能力。实验表明，即使是最先进的LLM在该基准上也面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗对话数据集缺乏长期时间线，无法支持跨会话推理评估；真实临床文本受隐私和伦理限制；现有基准仅聚焦孤立交互，无法捕捉跨会话的因果关系。

Method: 提出知识引导的三阶段合成框架：(1) 构建包含多样化疾病和并发症轨迹的合成患者档案；(2) 为每次就诊生成多轮对话；(3) 整合为连贯的纵向历史数据集MediLongChat。同时建立多维评估框架，包含自动指标（忠实性、连贯性、多样性）和LLM评估（正确性、真实性）。

Result: 即使在最先进的LLM上，MediLongChat的三个基准任务（对话内推理、跨对话推理、综合推理）仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: MediLongChat作为基准具有良好适用性，揭示了现有医疗智能体在长期记忆和推理能力上的不足，亟需针对性的方法改进。

Abstract: An effective healthcare agent must be able to recall and reason over a patient's longitudinal medical history. However, the absence of datasets with realistic long-term dialogue timelines limits systematic evaluation. Real clinical text is constrained by privacy and ethics, while existing benchmarks focus on isolated interactions, failing to capture cross-session reasoning. We introduce a framework for synthesizing high-quality, long-term medical dialogues with LLMs. Our approach entails a knowledge-guided decomposition into three stages: constructing synthetic patient profiles with diverse disease and complication trajectories, generating multi-turn dialogues per encounter, and integrating them into a coherent longitudinal history dataset, MediLongChat. We establish three benchmark tasks-In-dialogue Reasoning, Cross-dialogue Reasoning, and Synthesis Reasoning-to evaluate the memory capabilities of healthcare agents. To assess data quality, we introduce a multi-dimensional evaluation framework combining vector-based metrics with LLM-as-a-judge assessments. Specifically, we define automatic measures-Faithfulness, Coherence, and Diversity-together with two LLM-based evaluations: Correctness and Realism. Benchmark experiments show that even state-of-the-art LLMs struggle with MediLongChat. These findings highlight the benchmark's applicability and underscore the need for tailored methods to advance healthcare agents.

</details>


### [124] [Chunking German Legal Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19806)
*Max Prior,Natalia Milanova,Andreas Schultz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向德国民法典的检索增强生成分块策略，发现基于法律固有结构（章节、条款）的分块方法在召回率和计算效率上均优于语义聚类、RAPTOR等复杂方法，揭示了领域结构保留与语义增强之间的关键权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究法律文本检索增强生成中不同分块策略的效果，验证保留领域特定结构对法律信息检索的重要性。

Method: 以德国民法典为基准语料库，比较了结构性分块（章节、条款、句子、命题）、固定窗口、上下文分块、语义聚类、Lumber分块及RAPTOR分层检索等多种策略，在法律问答数据集上以章节级黄金标签评估召回率、查询延迟、索引构建时间和存储需求。

Result: 与法律固有结构对齐的分块策略（特别是章节和条款级别）实现了最高召回率，而覆盖这些结构的复杂方法性能更差；简单方法在计算效率上也优于LLM密集型技术（上下文分块、RAPTOR、Lumber）。

Conclusion: 保留领域特定结构对于有效的法律信息检索至关重要，语义增强与操作成本之间存在关键权衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates chunking strategies for retrieval-augmented generation on German statutory law, using the German Civil Code as a structured benchmark corpus. We implement and compare a range of segmentation approaches, including structural units (sections, subsections, sentences, propositions), fixed-size windows, contextual chunking, semantic clustering, Lumber-style chunking, and RAPTOR-based hierarchical retrieval. All methods are evaluated on a legal question-answering dataset with section-level gold labels, measuring recall, query latency, index build time, and storage requirements. Results show that chunking strategies aligned with the inherent legal structure - particularly section and subsection - based retrieval-achieve the highest recall, while more complex approaches that override this structure perform worse. These simpler methods also offer favorable computational efficiency compared to LLM-intensive techniques such as contextual chunking, RAPTOR, and Lumber. The findings highlight a key trade-off between semantic enrichment and operational cost, and demonstrate that preserving domain-specific structure is critical for effective legal information retrieval.

</details>


### [125] [LP-Eval: Rubric and Dataset for Measuring the Quality of Legal Proposition Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19815)
*Shanshan Xu,Johan Lindholm,Amogh Raina,Henrik Palmer Olsen,Daniel Hershcovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-Eval评估框架，研究利用大语言模型从欧盟法院判决中自动生成法律命题，发现模型能生成高质量命题，但专家评估显示基于成熟案例的命题优于新近案例，且LLM评估在细粒度区分上不如人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律命题生成是法律推理和学理研究的核心，但在法律自然语言处理领域尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统探究利用大语言模型自动生成和评估法律命题的可行性。

Method: 提出LP-Eval三步评估框架（形式有效性和实质性维度），与法律专家共同设计；构建100条LLM生成法律命题的双专家标注数据集；对比LLM自评估与人类专家评估。

Result: LLM能生成高质量法律命题；专家评估显示成熟案例的命题质量高于新近案例；基于评估标准引导的LLM评估比直接整体评分更接近人类专家，但对细粒度区分不敏感。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在法律命题生成任务上表现出色，但自动评估在捕捉专家级细粒度差异方面仍有局限，未来需进一步优化评估方法。

Abstract: Legal proposition generation is central to legal reasoning and doctrinal scholarship, yet remain under-examined in Legal NLP. This paper investigates the automatic generation and evaluation of legal propositions from decisions of the Court of Justice of the European Union using large language models (LLMs). We introduce LP-Eval, a three-step evaluation rubric co-designed with legal experts that decomposes legal proposition quality into formal validity and substantive dimensions. Using this rubric, we release a dataset of two experts' annotations for 100 LLM-generated legal propositions. Our results show that LLMs can generate predominantly well-formed and high-quality propositions, while expert evaluations reveal higher quality for propositions derived from well established cases than from recent ones. We further examine LLMs as evaluators and find that rubric-guided LLM judgments align more closely with expert assessments than direct overall scoring, but remain insensitive to finer-grained distinctions captured by human experts.

</details>


### [126] [Are Tools Always Beneficial? Learning to Invoke Tools Adaptively for Dual-Mode Multimodal LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19852)
*Qinghe Ma,Zhen Zhao,Yiming Wu,Jian Zhang,Lei Bai,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoTool 提出一种自适应决定是否调用外部工具的多模态大模型推理方法，在强化学习框架下通过双模式推理策略和模式特定奖励函数平衡工具辅助推理与纯文本推理，提升了推理准确率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强推理研究主要关注如何调用工具，但忽视了调用工具的必要性——冗余或不恰当的工具调用会增加推理开销甚至误导模型预测。

Method: 基于强化学习框架，设计显式的双模式推理策略（工具辅助推理与纯文本推理），并为每种模式设计独立的奖励函数；同时通过训练过程中联合探索与平衡两种推理模式，防止过早偏向单一模式，后期促进自由探索。

Result: 在 V* 基准上相比基座模型准确率提升 21.8%，在 POPE 基准上相比现有工具增强方法效率提升 44.9%。

Conclusion: AutoTool 能够根据查询特征自适应决定是否调用工具，在保持高性能的同时大幅提升推理效率，验证了选择性工具调用的重要性。

Abstract: Tool-augmented reasoning has emerged as a promising direction for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing studies mainly focus on enabling models to perform tool invocation, while neglecting the necessity of invoking tools. We argue that tool usage is not always beneficial, as redundant or inappropriate invocations largely increase reasoning overhead and even mislead model predictions. To address this issue, we introduce AutoTool, a model that adaptively decides whether to invoke tools according to the characteristics of each query. Within a reinforcement learning framework, we design an explicit dual-mode reasoning strategy with mode-specific reward functions to guide the model toward producing accurate responses. Moreover, to prevent premature bias toward a single reasoning mode, AutoTool jointly explores and balances tool-assisted and text-centric reasoning throughout training, and promotes free exploration in later stages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AutoTool exhibits outstanding performance and high efficiency, yielding a 21.8\% accuracy gain on V* benchmark compared to the base model, and a 44.9\% improvement in efficiency over existing tool-augmented methods on POPE benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/MQinghe/AutoTool.

</details>


### [127] [What Are LLMs Doing to Scientific Communication? Measuring Changes in Writing Practices and Reading Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19936)
*Filip Miletić,Neele Falk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分析ACL Anthology中37,000+篇论文（2020-2024）以及3,000段人工写作与LLM改进文本的对比，发现LLM的使用显著改变了学术写作风格：词汇频率与使用语境发生语义变化，LLM修改后的文本包含更多复杂句法结构、更长词汇和更低词汇多样性，专家认为这些文本更易理解、更令人兴奋，但对AI辅助写作持负面态度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在写作过程中的使用是否改变了科学交流的风格，特别是在自然语言处理领域。

Method: 构建两个数据集：ACL Anthology中37,000+篇论文的自然语料库（2020-2024）和3,000段人工写作与其LLM改进版本的合成数据集；进行历时词汇分析、复杂风格特征建模，以及20名领域专家的主观阅读体验标注研究。

Result: 词汇频率和使用语境随时间显著变化，出现语义特化或泛化现象；LLM修改文本更常包含特定句法结构、更复杂更长的词汇、更低词汇多样性；专家认为LLM改进文本更易理解和更令人兴奋，但对LLM持有负面态度。

Conclusion: LLM辅助写作对学术写作风格产生了可量化的影响，且AI辅助写作对阅读体验的影响具有强主观性。

Abstract: Has the style of scientific communication changed due to the growing use of large language models in the writing process? We address this question in the domain of Natural Language Processing by leveraging two data resources we create: a naturalistic corpus of over 37,000 papers from the ACL Anthology (2020-2024); and a synthetic dataset of 3,000 human-written passages and their LLM-generated improvements. We first implement a series of diachronic lexical analyses, showing that both word frequency and usage contexts have changed significantly over time, indicating semantic specialization in some cases and generalization in others. Broadening our perspective, we then model a range of more complex stylistic features and find that LLM-modified texts more frequently contain certain syntactic constructions, more complex and longer words and a lower lexical diversity. Finally, we connect these changes in writing practices to subjective reading experience through a pilot annotation study with 20 domain experts. They overall rate LLM-improved texts as more understandable and exciting, but also express negative qualitative attitudes towards LLMs, highlighting the strongly subjective effect of AI-assisted writing on reading experience.

</details>


### [128] [FlexDraft: Flexible Speculative Decoding via Attention Tuning and Bonus-Guided Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20022)
*Yaojie Zhang,Jianuo Huang,Junlong Ke,Yuhang Han,Yongji Long,Tianchen Zhao,Biqing Qi,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlexDraft 提出了一种无损推测解码框架，通过注意力微调、奖励引导校准和灵活解码三种设计，适应不同批量大小下的高效 LLM 推理，解决了并行推测解码中批量大时吞吐量崩溃的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行推测解码方法在小批量下有效，但在大批量下因奖励令牌与接受长度不确定性导致草稿验证不匹配，吞吐量增益崩溃；同时现有方法要么需要昂贵的持续预训练且质量下降，要么接受率低。

Method: (1) 注意力微调：仅微调最后几层注意力投影器，冻结自回归路径，实现块扩散式草稿生成，保留目标分布；(2) 奖励引导校准：基于已解决奖励令牌的轻量级 MLP 校准草稿 logits，缓解不确定性；(3) 灵活解码：小批量时并行草稿与验证，大批量时切换为顺序草稿再验证，并根据草稿置信度动态调整验证长度。

Result: 在小批量下保持并行推测解码的高效性，在大批量下避免吞吐量崩溃，且无需质量损失，可训练参数极少。

Conclusion: FlexDraft 通过三种互补设计，实现了跨批量大小的无损、自适应推测解码，有效解决了并行推测解码在大批量下的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates memory-bound LLM inference without quality degradation by using a fast drafter to propose multiple candidate tokens and the target model to verify them in parallel. However, conventional sequential speculative decoding suffers from mutual waiting between drafting and verification, and repeated exchange of intermediate states further increases memory access overhead. Parallel speculative decoding addresses this limitation by performing drafting and verification within a single target forward pass, allowing future drafts to be prepared while current candidates are being verified. Although effective at small batch sizes, existing parallel speculative decoding methods either require costly continual pretraining with quality degradation or suffer from low acceptance rates. More importantly, this paradigm inherently suffers from uncertainty in both the bonus token and the accepted length, leading to draft verification mismatch and causing throughput gains to collapse at large batch sizes. To address these limitations, we introduce FlexDraft, a lossless speculative decoding framework that flexibly adapts to varying batch sizes through three key designs. (1) Attention Tuning enables block diffusion drafting by tuning only the attention projectors of the final few layers on mask tokens, while keeping the autoregressive path frozen to preserve the target distribution and produce high quality drafts with minimal trainable parameters. (2) Bonus-guided Calibration uses a lightweight MLP conditioned on the resolved bonus token to calibrate draft logits, mitigating draft verification mismatch caused by bonus token uncertainty. (3) Flex Decoding dynamically switches between parallel draft and verify at small batch sizes and sequential draft then verify at large batch sizes, and adjusts verification length based on draft confidence to eliminate redundant computation.

</details>


### [129] [Mind Your Moras: Orthography-Aware Error Analysis of Neural Japanese Morphological Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20043)
*Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以日语过去时态形态变化为研究对象，提出了一种正字法感知的错误分析方法，将平假名视为编码形态音位差异的表征系统。在两种字符级序列到序列架构上评估了模型表现，发现尽管总体准确率较高，但模型表现出系统性的、语言学上可解释的错误，这些错误集中在平假名的特定正字法属性上。引入了一个包含7种主要失败模式的错误分类体系，其中促音相关错误占残差错误的75-80%，尤其出现在以元音e结尾且需要在过去时后缀前促音化的动词词干上。错误模式在不同架构和随机种子间高度一致，表明正字法表征、形态结构和数据频率效应之间存在稳健的交互作用。这些结果强调了正字法感知的评估对于理解神经模型在形态复杂语言中的泛化能力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对神经模型在形态变化任务上的评估往往忽视正字法系统的语言学本质。日语平假名不仅是语音转录工具，更是一个编码形态音位区别的表征系统，可能影响模型的泛化行为。作者旨在揭示正字法如何塑造神经模型的错误模式，而非简单计算准确率。

Method: 采用两种字符级序列到序列架构，在SIGMORPHON 2020和2023共享任务格式的数据集上进行日语过去时态形态变化实验。引入了一个包含7种主要失败模式的错误分类体系，对模型输出进行定量和定性分析。

Result: 尽管总体准确率较高，模型错误具有系统性且语言学可解释；促音相关错误占残差错误的75-80%，尤其集中于以元音e结尾且需要促音化的动词词干；错误模式在不同架构和随机种子间高度一致。

Conclusion: 正字法表征、形态结构和数据频率效应之间存在稳健的交互作用，共同塑造模型的泛化行为。正字法感知的评估对于理解神经模型在形态复杂语言中的泛化能力不可或缺。

Abstract: We present an orthography-aware error analysis of Japanese past-tense morphological inflection, treating hiragana not merely as a transcriptional medium, but as a representational system encoding morphophonological distinctions that may influence model generalization. We evaluate two character-level sequence-to-sequence architectures on past-tense formation using datasets formatted according to the SIGMORPHON 2020 and 2023 shared task conventions. Despite high aggregate accuracy, models exhibit systematic, linguistically interpretable errors that cluster around specific orthographic properties of hiragana. We introduce a concise error taxonomy capturing seven primary failure modes and provide both quantitative and qualitative analyses. Gemination-related errors dominate residual failures, accounting for 75-80% of errors, particularly in verbs whose stems end in the vowel e and require gemination before the past-tense suffix. Error patterns remain highly consistent across architectures and random seeds, suggesting a robust interaction between orthographic representation, morphological structure, and data frequency effects in shaping model generalization. These results underscore the necessity of orthography-aware evaluation for understanding neural generalization in morphologically complex languages.

</details>


### [130] [Language Mutations Sustain the Persistences of Conspiracy Theories on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20050)
*Calvin Yixiang Cheng,Dorian Quelle,Scott A. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究基于三年间X平台（原Twitter）上关于阴谋论的帖子数据，通过计算语言学分析和生存建模，发现语义突变越大的阴谋论主张存活时间越长。心理语言学属性（代词、社交指代词、认知过程词、风险/健康词汇）和AAT（行动者-行动-目标）类别的突变均与更长的生命周期显著相关。定性分析识别出两种主要突变模式：简化与同化。研究建议内容审核应关注阴谋论的核心主张及可变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言突变如何影响阴谋论在社交媒体上的持续传播，填补现有研究对阴谋论变异机制及其对持久性影响的认知空白。

Method: 基于X平台三年期阴谋论相关帖子数据集，运用计算语言学分析与生存建模（Survival Modelling），结合定性分析识别突变模式。

Result: 语义突变越大的阴谋论主张生命周期越长；心理语言学属性（代词、社交指代词、认知过程词、风险/健康词汇）及AAT（行动者-行动-目标）类别突变与延长寿命显著相关；发现简化与同化两种主要突变模式。

Conclusion: 语言突变是阴谋论在线持续传播的关键机制；内容审核策略应关注阴谋论核心主张的可变性，而非仅针对固定表述。

Abstract: This study investigates how language mutations affect the persistent diffusion of conspiracy theories on social media. Drawing on a three-year dataset of conspiracy-related posts from X, and applying computational linguistic analysis alongside survival modelling, we find that conspiracy claims with greater semantic mutations have substantially longer lifespans. Mutations in psycholinguistic properties, including pronouns, social reference words, cognitive process terms, risk- and health- related vocabularies, are associated with extended lifespans. Mutations in actor, action and target (AAT) categories are associated with longer lifespans as well. Qualitative analysis identifies two predominant mutation patterns: simplification and assimilation, at both linguistic and AAT structural levels. Taken together, the results advance our understanding of how language mutations contribute to conspiracy persistence online and shed lights on longitudinal content moderation strategies. We argue that content moderation should consider the mutability of conspiracy claims and focus on the core claims that can address their potential variations.

</details>


### [131] [PromptRad: Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Label Prompt-Tuning for Low-Resource Radiology Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20052)
*Ying-Jia Lin,Tzu-Chin Lo,Ping-Chien Li,Chi-Tung Cheng,Chien-Hung Liao,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PromptRad 是一种知识增强的提示微调方法，针对低资源场景下的放射学报告多标签分类问题。它通过将多标签分类重构为掩码语言建模，并整合 UMLS Metathesaurus 中的同义词到多词动词化器中，仅需少量标注数据即可实现优于传统方法的性能，并在肝脏 CT 报告上以 32 个标注样本超越字典方法和微调基线，与 GPT-4 性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的报告标注方法难以处理临床报告中多样化的描述，而微调预训练语言模型需要大量标注数据，这在临床环境中往往难以获取。因此需要一种在低资源场景下有效的放射学报告标注方法。

Method: PromptRad 采用提示微调方法，将多标签分类重构为掩码语言建模任务；利用 UMLS Metathesaurus 中的同义词构建多词动词化器，丰富类别语义表示；不添加额外分类层，直接微调 PLM。

Result: 在肝脏 CT 报告上，仅需 32 个标注训练样本即可超越基于字典和传统微调的基线方法；使用更小的模型即可达到与 GPT-4 竞争的性能；能更有效地捕捉复杂否定模式。

Conclusion: PromptRad 是一种在数据稀缺临床场景下有前景的报告标注解决方案，兼顾了低资源需求和强性能表现。

Abstract: Automatic report labeling facilitates the identification of clinical findings from unstructured text and enables large-scale annotation for medical imaging research. Existing rule-based labelers struggle with the diverse descriptions in clinical reports, while fine-tuning pre-trained language models (PLMs) requires large amounts of labeled data that are often unavailable in clinical settings. In this paper, we propose PromptRad, a knowledge-enhanced multi-label \textbf{prompt}-tuning approach for \textbf{rad}iology report labeling under low-resource settings. PromptRad reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and incorporates synonyms from the UMLS Metathesaurus into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich category representations. By fine-tuning the PLM without additional classification layers, PromptRad requires substantially less labeled data than conventional fine-tuning. Experiments on liver CT reports show that PromptRad outperforms dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines with only 32 labeled training examples, and achieves competitive performance with GPT-4 despite using a much smaller model. Further analysis demonstrates that PromptRad captures complex negation patterns more effectively than existing methods, making it a promising solution for report labeling in data-scarce clinical scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ila-lab/PromptRad.

</details>


### [132] [Rewarding Beliefs, Not Actions: Consistency-Guided Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20061)
*Wenjie Tang,Minne Li,Sijie Huang,Liquan Xiao,Yuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReBel算法，通过显式建模结构化信念状态来解决部分可观测环境下长程交互任务中的时序信用分配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上相比GRPO提升最高20.4%的成功率，并实现2.1倍的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，智能体信念随时间漂移且奖励延迟，导致时序信用分配困难，现有RLVR方法缺乏对信念状态的显式建模与利用。

Method: 提出ReBel算法：1) 显式建模结构化信念状态以总结交互历史；2) 信念一致性监督，将预测信念与观测反馈的差异转化为密集自监督信号；3) 信念感知分组，在相似信念状态下比较轨迹以得到更鲁棒的低方差优势估计。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，ReBel相比GRPO基线任务成功率提升最高20.4个百分点，样本效率提升2.1倍。

Conclusion: 信念感知的自监督是部分可观测条件下长程决策的有效方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a promising paradigm for improving large language model (LLM) agents on long-horizon interactive tasks. However, in partially observable environments, incomplete observations cause agent beliefs to drift over time, while delayed rewards obscure the causal impact of intermediate decisions, exacerbating temporal credit assignment challenges. To address this, we propose ReBel (Reward Belief), a process-level reinforcement learning algorithm that explicitly models structured belief states to summarize interaction history and guide subsequent policy learning. ReBel introduces belief-consistency supervision, converting discrepancies between predicted beliefs and observed feedback into dense self-supervised signals without requiring external step-wise annotations or verifiers. It also employs belief-aware grouping to compare trajectories under similar belief states, yielding more robust and lower-variance advantage estimates. We evaluate ReBel on challenging long-horizon benchmarks, including ALFWorld and WebShop. ReBel improves task success by up to $20.4$ percentage points over the episode-level baseline GRPO and increases sample efficiency by $2.1\times$. These results suggest that belief-aware self-supervision is a promising direction for reliable long-horizon decision-making under partial observability. Code is available at: https://github.com/Fateyetian/Rebel.git.

</details>


### [133] [Text-to-SPARQL Generation with Reinforcement Learning: A GRPO-based Approach on DBLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20066)
*Jann Pfeifer,Debayan Banerjee,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究使用基于结果奖励的强化学习（GRPO）训练小型指令微调模型（Qwen3-1.7B）在学术领域进行零样本Text-to-SPARQL生成，在DBLP-QuAD数据集上，与零样本基线和有监督DoRA微调基线相比，GRPO显著提升性能，其中执行奖励贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问答方法依赖大型模型或完整监督（金标准查询标注），本研究探索在缺乏金标准查询标注的情况下，能否通过基于结果奖励的强化学习训练小型语言模型实现零样本Text-to-SPARQL生成。

Method: 采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）应用于Qwen3-1.7B模型，在DBLP-QuAD数据集上使用自然语言问题与实体/关系符号提示结合的prompt，训练依赖执行反馈、结构约束和答案级奖励，并包含使用金标准查询塑形的变体。

Result: GRPO显著优于零样本基线并具有竞争性的泛化能力；有监督DoRA微调在相同模型规模上取得更高总体准确率；消融分析表明执行奖励贡献最大，塑形奖励的额外收益有限。

Conclusion: 基于结果奖励的强化学习在缺乏金标准查询进行词元级监督时是一种可行的训练策略。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering seeks to translate natural language questions into executable queries over knowledge graphs, but existing approaches often rely on large models or full supervision in the form of gold query annotations. This study examines whether reinforcement learning with outcome-based rewards can train a small instruction-tuned language model to perform zero-shot Text-to-SPARQL generation in the scholarly domain. Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is applied to the Qwen3-1.7B model on DBLP-QuAD, using prompts that combine natural language questions with symbolic hints about entities and relations. Training relies on execution feedback, structural constraints, and answer-level rewards, with an additional variant that incorporates gold-query-based shaping. The resulting models are compared to the unmodified zero-shot baseline and to a supervised DoRA-finetuned baseline across answer-level accuracy, execution accuracy, category-wise scores, and generalization to held-out templates. GRPO substantially improves over the zero-shot baseline and exhibits competitive generalization, while supervised DoRA finetuning achieves higher overall accuracy on the same model scale. Ablation analyses indicate that execution-based rewards account for most gains, with additional shaping yielding limited additional benefit, suggesting that outcome-based reinforcement learning is a viable training strategy when gold queries are unavailable for token-level supervision.

</details>


### [134] [CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20075)
*Dachuan Shi,Hanlin Zhu,Xiangchi Yuan,Wanjia Zhao,Kejing Xia,Wen Xiao,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CopT，一种反转思考与回答顺序的推理流程：先产生草稿答案，再基于草稿进行反思修正。通过对比离散token输入与连续embedding输入下的模型支持度，构建逆KL散度估计器评估答案可靠性，并动态控制草稿答案在后续思考中的可见性。在数学、代码和智能体推理任务上，CopT在不需额外训练的情况下，峰值准确率提升最多23%，token使用量降低最多57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准思维链（CoT）将思考视为回答的前提，即使模型能快速识别答案也需进行冗长的推理步骤，导致不必要的token开销和回答延迟，这种现象称为"表演性推理"。作者希望设计一种先回答后反思的推理流程，避免不必要的思考成本，同时保持甚至提升推理质量。

Method: CopT包含三个关键组件：1) 草稿答案生成：首先让模型直接产生一个初步答案；2) 推理时对比验证器：将连续embedding输入下模型对生成token的支持度与离散token输入下的支持度进行对比，计算序列级别的逆KL散度估计值作为答案可靠性指标；3) 基于策略的反思推理：当可靠性不足时，启动后续思考过程，并通过第二个KL估计器动态控制草稿答案在思考过程中的可见性，保留有用信息同时防止被不可靠内容误导。理论上证明了该估计量的期望等于未解析隐状态与答案token之间的互信息。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和智能体推理等多个任务上，CopT相比标准CoT方法，峰值准确率提升最多23%，token使用量降低最多57%，且在达到相当或更高准确率的前提下实现了显著的计算效率提升。所有改进均无需额外的模型训练。

Conclusion: CopT通过反转思考与回答的顺序，有效解决了标准CoT中的表演性推理问题，在不牺牲推理质量的前提下大幅降低计算开销。其推理时对比验证器为评估答案可靠性提供了理论支撑，动态可见性控制机制则平衡了利用已有信息与被误导的风险。该方法为提升LLM推理效率开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is a standard approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities from large language models (LLMs). However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of thinking and answering. Instead of thinking before answering, CopT first elicits a draft answer and then invokes subsequent on-policy thinking conditioned on its own draft answer for reflection and correction. To assess whether the draft answer should be trusted, CopT recasts continuous embeddings as inference-time contrastive verifiers. Specifically, it contrasts the model's support for the same generated tokens under discrete-token inputs and continuous-embedding inputs, yielding a sequence-level reverse KL estimator for answer reliability. Our analysis shows that under certain assumptions, the expected estimate equals the mutual information between the unresolved latent state and the emitted answer token, explaining why it captures answer-relevant uncertainty rather than arbitrary uncertainty in the latent state. When the answer is deemed insufficiently reliable, CopT performs further on-policy thinking, where a second KL estimator dynamically controls draft-answer visibility, preserving useful partial information while reducing the risk of being misled by unreliable content. Across mathematics, coding, and agentic reasoning tasks, CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and reduces token usage by up to 57% at comparable or higher accuracy, without any additional training. The code is available at https://github.com/sdc17/CopT.

</details>


### [135] [ThoughtTrace: Understanding User Thoughts in Real-World LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20087)
*Chuanyang Jin,Binze Li,Haopeng Xie,Cathy Mengying Fang,Tianjian Li,Shayne Longpre,Hongxiang Gu,Maximillian Chen,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ThoughtTrace是首个大规模数据集，记录了用户在真实多轮人机对话中的内心想法（发送提示的原因及对助手响应的反应），包含1,058名用户、2,155场对话、17,058轮次和10,174条想法标注，涵盖20种语言模型。分析表明想法在语义上不同于消息、前沿LLM难以从上下文推断、内容多样且与对话阶段相关。想法可提升用户行为预测，并为训练个性化助手提供细粒度对齐信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话数据集仅捕捉用户说了什么，而非用户内心想法，缺乏对用户潜在目标、偏好和需求的认知理解。

Method: 收集真实多轮人机对话中用户自我报告的想法（prompt动机与对响应的反应），构建包含1,058名用户、2,155场对话、17,058轮次和10,174条标注的大规模数据集，覆盖20种语言模型。

Result: 想法与消息在语义上存在显著差异；前沿LLM难以从对话上下文中准确推断用户想法；想法内容多样且与对话阶段相关；作为推理时上下文可提升用户行为预测性能；想法引导的改写为训练个性化助手提供细粒度对齐信号。

Conclusion: ThoughtTrace将用户想法确立为研究人机交互认知动态的新数据模态，为构建能更好理解和适应用户潜在目标、偏好与需求的助手奠定基础。

Abstract: Conversational AI has now reached billions of users, yet existing datasets capture only what people say, not what they think. We introduce ThoughtTrace, the first large-scale dataset that pairs real-world multi-turn human--AI conversations with users' self-reported thoughts: their reasons for sending prompts and reactions to assistant responses. ThoughtTrace comprises 1,058 users, 2,155 conversations, 17,058 turns, and 10,174 thought annotations collected across 20 language models. Our analysis shows that ThoughtTrace captures long-horizon, topically diverse interactions, and that thoughts are semantically distinct from messages, difficult for frontier LLMs to infer from context, diverse in content, and tied to conversation stages. We further demonstrate the utility of thoughts for downstream modeling. First, thoughts improve user-behavior prediction as inference-time context. Second, thought-guided rewrites provide fine-grained alignment signals for training personalized assistants. Together, ThoughtTrace establishes user thoughts as a new data modality for studying the cognitive dynamics behind human--AI interaction and provides a foundation for building assistants that better understand and adapt to users' latent goals, preferences, and needs.

</details>


### [136] [MixRea: Benchmarking Explicit-Implicit Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20128)
*Yuanqing Cai,Ziyi Huang,Minhao Liu,Lixin Duan,Wen Li,Yanru Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出显式-隐式推理任务及MixRea基准，发现大语言模型存在类似人类"非注意盲视"的认知缺陷（即使最佳模型Gemini 2.5 Pro仅42.8%一致性），并提出潜在关系补全提示方法PRCP来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类认知中"非注意盲视"理论启发，探究大语言模型在显性任务指令下是否会忽视微妙但重要的上下文线索，即训练于嵌入注意力偏见的人类偏好语料库的LLMs是否具有类似的局限性。

Method: 1) 提出显式-隐式推理任务并构建MixRea基准（2,246道多选题，涵盖9种推理类型，显式/隐式信息分布各异）；2) 评估21个先进LLM的性能；3) 提出潜在关系补全提示方法，通过恢复被忽略的因果关联来改进推理。

Result: 即使表现最好的推理模型Gemini 2.5 Pro也仅达到42.8%的一致性，揭示了大语言模型中普遍存在的非注意盲视现象。

Conclusion: LLMs存在类似人类非注意盲视的认知局限，且该局限在多种多源推理任务中持续存在，表明需要开发更具认知对齐能力的模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into high-stakes decision-making. Inspired by the theory of \emph{inattentional blindness} in human cognition, we investigate whether LLMs, trained on human-preferred corpora that embed attentional biases, exhibit a similar limitation: \emph{failing to attend to subtle yet important contextual cues under explicit task instructions}. To evaluate this, we introduce the task of \textbf{explicit-implicit reasoning} and present \textbf{MixRea}, a benchmark of 2,246 multiple-choice questions across 9 reasoning types with varying distributions of explicit and implicit information. Evaluation of 21 advanced LLMs shows that even the best-performing reasoning model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieves only 42.8\% consistency, revealing widespread inattentional blindness. To mitigate this, we propose \textbf{Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP)}, a prompting method that improves reasoning by recovering overlooked causal relations. Further analysis shows that this limitation persists across diverse multi-source reasoning tasks, highlighting the need for more cognitively aligned models.

</details>


### [137] [Less Back-and-Forth: A Comparative Study of Structured Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20149)
*Saurav Ghosh,Gabriella Polach,Abdou Sow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化提示设计对LLM响应质量的影响，发现清单改进提示在评分（7.50/8）和效率上均优于原始提示（5.67）和澄清问题提示（6.67）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在开放式任务中常因提示不明确导致低质量回答和额外交互，本文旨在探索结构化提示设计是否能在提升响应质量的同时减少用户努力。

Method: 比较三种提示条件（原始提示、清单改进提示、澄清问题提示），在四类任务（摘要、规划、解释、编码）上使用三个LLM系统（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok）进行评估，采用统一评分标准（任务完成度、正确性、合规性、清晰度）。

Result: 清单改进提示平均评分7.50/8，优于原始提示（5.67）和澄清问题提示（6.67）；同时使用更少的平均token数量，实现了最佳质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 简单的提示清单可以有效提升LLM响应质量，同时减少不必要的交互。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for open-ended tasks, but underspecified prompts can lead to low-quality answers and additional interaction. This paper studies whether structured prompt design improves response quality while reducing user effort. We compare three prompt conditions: a raw prompt, a checklist-improved prompt, and a clarifying-question prompt. We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.

</details>


### [138] [ClinSeekAgent: Automating Multimodal Evidence Seeking for Agentic Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20176)
*Juncheng Wu,Letian Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Haoqin Tu,Hardy Chen,Zijun Wang,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClinSeekAgent 是一个面向临床决策的智能体框架，能够主动从多种数据源（医学知识库、电子病历、医学影像）中动态检索、迭代推理并整合多模态证据，实现从被动证据消费到主动证据获取的范式转变。在文本和多模态任务上均显著提升多种大模型的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床决策支持系统大多假设证据已被预先整理好并直接提供给模型，而真实临床工作流要求智能体能够主动从异构数据源中检索、迭代规划并综合多模态证据。

Method: 构建ClinSeekAgent智能体框架，支持两种模式：1) 推理时智能体：直接为前沿LLM提供多步证据检索、假设细化与整合能力；2) 训练时蒸馏管道：将高质量智能体轨迹蒸馏到紧凑型开源模型中。同时构建ClinSeek-Bench基准测试，包含固定预选证据的Curated Input推理和原始临床数据的Automated Evidence-Seeking两种评测模式。

Result: 文本EHR任务：Claude Opus 4.6 F1从60.0提升至63.2，MiniMax M2.5从43.1提升至47.3，9个模型中有7个在风险预测上获得正向增益。多模态任务：Claude Opus 4.6 F1从47.5提升至62.6（+15.1），所有模型在三项CXR相关任务组上均有提升。蒸馏后的ClinSeek-35B-A3B在AgentEHR-Bench上达到34.0平均F1，较Qwen3.5-35B-A3B基线提升+11.9分，接近Claude Opus 4.6水平。

Conclusion: ClinSeekAgent验证了主动证据获取范式在临床决策支持中的有效性，既可作为推理时智能体提升前沿模型性能，也可作为训练管道增强小型开源模型能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems have shown promise for clinical decision support, but existing works largely assume that evidence has already been curated and handed to the model. Real-world clinical workflows instead require agents to actively seek, iteratively plan, and synthesize multimodal evidence from heterogeneous sources. In this paper, we introduce ClinSeekAgent, an automated agentic framework for dynamic multimodal evidence seeking that shifts the paradigm from passive evidence consumption to active evidence acquisition. Given only a clinical query and access to raw data sources, ClinSeekAgent gathers evidence by querying medical knowledge bases, navigating raw EHRs, and invoking medical imaging tools; refines its hypotheses as new information emerges; and integrates the collected evidence into grounded clinical decisions. ClinSeekAgent serves both as an inference-time agent for frontier LLMs and as a training-time pipeline for distilling high-quality agent trajectories into compact open-source models. To validate its inference-time effectiveness, we construct ClinSeek-Bench, which pairs Curated Input reasoning from fixed pre-selected evidence with Automated Evidence-Seeking over raw clinical data. On text-only EHR tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 60.0 to 63.2 overall F1 and MiniMax M2.5 from 43.1 to 47.3, with positive risk-prediction gains in 7 out of 9 evaluated host models. On multimodal tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 47.5 to 62.6 (+15.1); all evaluated models improve across the three CXR-related task groups. We further validate ClinSeekAgent as a training pipeline by distilling agentic evidence-seeking trajectories into ClinSeek-35B-A3B, which achieves 34.0 average F1 on existing AgentEHR-Bench, improving over its Qwen3.5-35B-A3B baseline by +11.9 points and approaching Claude Opus 4.6.

</details>


### [139] [From Seeing to Thinking: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning Improves Post-Training of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20177)
*Juncheng Wu,Hardy Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xianfeng Tang,Freda Shi,Hui Liu,Hanqing Lu,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）后训练中感知与推理的交互关系，提出将能力分解为视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理三个训练阶段。实验表明：视觉感知需要针对性优化且作为基础支撑，通过分阶段训练（先固化感知，再精炼推理）比混合训练效果更优，且强化学习比基于字幕的SFT更有效。该方法在多个VLM上实现1.5%推理精度提升和20.8%推理轨迹缩短，并结合难度课程学习获得进一步增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM虽强调长链思维推理，但其在视觉任务上的性能瓶颈主要源于视觉感知不足而非推理能力本身，因此需要系统探究感知与推理在VLM后训练中的相互作用机制。

Method: 将VLM能力分解为视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理三个独立训练阶段，分别配备专门的训练数据；采用分阶段训练策略，先通过RL强化视觉感知根基，再进行视觉推理精炼；并与基于难度的课程学习进行正交组合。

Result: 分阶段训练一致优于混合训练，实现1.5%推理精度提升和20.8%推理轨迹缩短；在WeMath上提升5.2%，在RealWorldQA上提升3.7%；能力分阶段与难度课程学习正交组合可获得加性增益。

Conclusion: 视觉感知是VLM性能的基础瓶颈，需要通过分阶段训练和RL来针对性优化；更好的感知可以显著减少不必要的长链推理，表明感知与推理之间存在能力互补关系。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) emphasize long chain-of-thought reasoning; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training data. We demonstrate that visual perception (a) requires targeted optimization with specialized data; (b) serves as a fundamental scaffold that should be solidified through staged training before refining visual reasoning; and (c) is more effectively learned via RL than caption-based SFT. Our experiments across multiple VLMs demonstrate that staged training consistently improves both visual perception and reasoning performance over merged training. Notably, models trained with our approach achieve 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy with 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, suggesting that superior perception reduces the need for excessive reasoning. Furthermore, we show that this capability-based staging represents a new curriculum dimension orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, and combining both yields further additive gains. Our staged-training models achieve superior performance among open-weight VLMs, establishing advanced results on several visual math and perception (e.g., +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA) tasks compared with the base counterpart.

</details>


### [140] [TIDE: Efficient and Lossless MoE Diffusion LLM Inference with I/O-aware Expert Offload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20179)
*Zhiben Chen,Youpeng Zhao,Yang Sui,Jun Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIDE是一个面向扩散大语言模型的资源高效推理系统，利用块内扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，通过基于间隔的I/O感知专家刷新策略和数学规划求解最优调度，在不需模型训练的情况下实现无损加速，在GPU-CPU系统上相比基线方法获得最高1.5倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）作为自回归模型的竞争替代方案，具有更好的硬件利用率和双向上下文能力，但随着其采用混合专家（MoE）架构不断扩展，在资源受限设备上的部署面临挑战，现有方法存在严重的I/O开销或计算瓶颈。

Method: 利用块内扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，提出基于间隔的I/O感知专家刷新策略；将推理调度建模为数学规划问题，求解最小化I/O流量和CPU计算的最优间隔。

Result: 在单GPU-CPU系统上，TIDE在LLaDA2.0-mini和LLaDA2.0-flash模型上分别实现相比基线方法最高1.4倍和1.5倍的吞吐提升。

Conclusion: TIDE是一种无损优化方法，无需模型训练即可显著加速dLLM推理，为扩散大语言模型在资源受限设备上的高效部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding. However, as dLLMs continue to scale up with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, their deployment on resource-constrained devices remains an open challenge. Existing AR-based methods often incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks. In this work, we propose TIDE, a novel resource-efficient inference system that leverages the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block. Specifically, we leverage the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block and introduce an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates the expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion. To ensure optimal performance, we formulate the inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem, solving for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation. Most importantly, TIDE is a lossless optimization that requires no model training, providing a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference. In a single GPU-CPU system, we demonstrate that TIDE achieves up to 1.4$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.

</details>


### [141] [IMLJD: A Computational Dataset for Indian Matrimonial Litigation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19346)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍IMLJD数据集，包含3,613份印度婚姻纠纷判决书（2000-2024年最高法院1,474份，2018-2024年卡纳塔克邦高等法院2,139份），发现最高法院驳回申请成功率（57.6%）显著高于高等法院（39.7%），并在时间匹配后差异扩大至19.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏结构化的印度婚姻纠纷判决数据集，难以系统分析不同层级法院对家庭暴力等案件的处理差异。

Method: 构建包含结构化结果标签、元数据指标和知识图谱的数据集，覆盖最高法院（2000-2024）和卡纳塔克邦高等法院（2018-2024）的判决，并进行时间匹配分析。

Result: 最高法院驳回申请成功率57.6%，高等法院39.7%；时间匹配后（2018-2024）最高法院为59.3%，差异扩大至19.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 印度最高法院和高等法院在婚姻纠纷驳回申请的成功率存在显著且稳健的差异，数据集已开源。

Abstract: We present IMLJD, an open dataset of 3,613 Indian court judgments covering matrimonial disputes under IPC Section 498A, the Protection of Women from Domestic Violence Act, and CrPC Section 482. The dataset covers the Supreme Court of India from 2000 to 2024 (1,474 cases) and the Karnataka High Court from 2018 to 2024 (2,139 cases), with structured outcome labels, metadata-derived indicators, and a knowledge graph. We find that 57.6% of quashing petitions succeed at the Supreme Court level compared to 39.7% at the Karnataka High Court level. On a matched 2018 to 2024 period, the SC quash rate is 59.3%, widening the differential to 19.6 percentage points and confirming the finding is robust to temporal adjustment. The dataset, code, and knowledge graph are released openly at https://github.com/joyboseroy/imljd and https://huggingface.co/datasets/joyboseroy/imljd.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出系统性生成数据探针（data probes）的方法，即从随机过程中合成序列，用于研究数据特性如何影响大语言模型在训练、微调、对齐、上下文学习等各阶段的表现，从而超越经验启发式方法，建立数据作用的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对数据如何影响LLM各阶段表现的理解依赖于大量实验和公共数据集的经验启发式方法，计算成本高且缺乏系统性和理论原则。作者倡导发展系统性的方法论来揭示数据特性驱动LLM行为的本质。

Method: 提出从适当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列（数据探针），这些序列可在LLM工作流的一个或多个阶段使用，通过观察LLM在数据探针上的行为，系统研究数据特性对模型性能、泛化和鲁棒性的影响。利用典型集等理论概念描述探针序列的统计特性。

Result: 数据探针方法为揭示数据在LLM训练和推理中的基础性作用提供了路径，可超越经验启发式，建立系统性研究框架。

Conclusion: 发展系统性的数据探针方法论有助于建立数据影响LLM行为的理论基础，是理解数据本质作用的关键方向。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [143] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种微服务架构，将分类、OCR和LLM结构化字段提取等多个模型封装成管道，支持每小时处理数千份多页文档，并分享了生产级部署的设计经验与性能发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术研究偏向于提出新的文档理解模型，导致模型定义与生产级大规模运行之间存在巨大空白。本文旨在填补这一鸿沟。

Method: 采用微服务架构封装多模型管道，包含混合分类、GPU推理与CPU编排分离、异步IO处理以及独立水平扩展策略。

Result: 通过批处理性能分析发现两个关键结果：OCR而非语言模型解析主导端到端延迟；系统并发饱和度由共享GPU推理容量决定，而非工作线程数量。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供了超越基准测试、可实际生产部署的文档理解系统架构模式。

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [144] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LBW-Guard 是一个运行在 AdamW 优化器之上的有界自主训练管控层，通过观测训练遥测数据并施加有界控制，在不替换优化器的情况下提升大语言模型训练的稳定性。在 Qwen2.5 系列模型上的实验表明，该方法在降低困惑度、加速训练以及应对高学习率压力方面均显著优于 AdamW 和梯度裁剪基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练在激进的学习率、规模扩展和运行时压力条件下，越来越容易遭遇训练不稳定、训练退化以及计算资源浪费的问题，亟需一种不依赖替换优化器或局部梯度抑制的轻量级稳定性治理方案。

Method: LBW-Guard 作为一个位于 AdamW 优化器之上的有界自主训练管控层，通过实时观测训练遥测数据，识别对不稳定性敏感的训练区间，并在保持固定训练目标的前提下对优化器执行施加有界控制，而非替换优化器的更新规则。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B 上，LBW-Guard 将最终困惑度从 13.21 降至 10.74（提升 18.7%），端到端时间从 392.54s 降至 357.02s（1.10x 加速）。在高学习率压力下（LR=3e-3），AdamW 退化至 1885.24 困惑度，而 LBW-Guard 仍保持在 11.57。梯度裁剪基线无法复现该效果。

Conclusion: 论文得出一个系统层面的结论：对稳定性敏感的大语言模型训练可以从优化器之上的治理平面中受益。LBW-Guard 证明，有界运行时控制能够在压力条件下保持有效计算，且这种方法与优化器替换和局部梯度抑制有本质区别。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [145] [AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
