*Olena Bogdanov,Yeunji Jung,Chandra Dhir,Pareekshitreddy Gaddam,Saurabh Jain,Lakshmi Tumati,Vijay Parthasarathy,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体NL2SQL方法，通过语义增强的schema表示和用户业务规则，在BIRD基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率。核心贡献包括：优化编排器实现规划-反射-自纠正、上下文感知的schema enrichment方法，以及跨领域泛化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能力快速进步，NL2SQL在准确性上仍未达到人类专家SQL编写者的水平，需要进一步的算法改进。

Method: 提出多智能体方法：(a) 设计优化编排器，利用LLM进行规划、编排、反射和自纠正生成SQL；(b) 开发高级schema enrichment方法，创建上下文感知的元数据；(c) 加入用户提供的业务规则。

Result: 在BIRD基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率，并在不同领域和数据集上验证了准确性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了NL2SQL的准确性，且具有良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) conversion is an important problem for researchers and enterprises due to the ubiquitous importance of relational databases in broad-ranging practical problems. Despite the rapid advancements in the capabilities of LLMs, NL2SQL has not reached parity in accuracy with human expert SQL writers, hence needing additional improvements in NL2SQL algorithms. This study presents a new multi-agent method for NL2SQL that achieves 78.1% semantic accuracy on the BIg Bench for LaRge-scale Database (BIRD) benchmark. Our method leverages a semantically enriched representation of user-provided schema, adds user-provided business rules, and produces accurate SQL queries. The main contributions of this study are (a) We designed an optimized new orchestrator in a multi-agent solution that uses LLMs to plan, orchestrate, reflect, and self-correct to generate accurate SQL queries, (b) We developed an advanced schema enrichment method that creates context-aware metadata to improve accuracy, and (c) We demonstrated the accuracy and generalizability of the method across different domains and datasets by evaluating it on the BIRD-SQL benchmark.

</details>


### [146] [DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu,Zijian Carl Ma,Ao Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DecisionBench，一个用于评估长时程智能体工作流中涌现委派（emergent delegation）能力的基准平台。平台固定任务套件、模型池、委派接口、确定性技能标注层和多轴评价指标集。基于11个模型、23,375个实例的五条件参考扫描表明：不同感知条件下终端任务质量无显著差异，但路由保真度差异显著（7.5%-29.5%），且完美委派的上限空间尚有15-31个百分点的未开发余地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法对智能体在长时程工作流中的委派能力（即何时将任务路由给其他模型）进行隔离测量与系统评估，导致仅关注终端任务质量会掩盖底层的编排信号。

Method: 设计了一个模块化基准平台，固定四项要素（任务套件、模型池、委派接口、技能标注层），并定义多轴评价指标体系（质量、成本、延迟、委派率、路由保真度@K、供应商自偏好、反事实委派上限）。在11个模型构成的完整池上执行五条件参考扫描（n=23,375），对感知信息的内容与投递通道进行控制实验。

Result: (i) 四种感知条件下的平均终端任务质量统计上无显著差异（|beta|<=0.010, p>=0.21）；(ii) 路由保真度@1在条件间波动于7.5%-29.5%，投递通道（按需工具 vs. 预载描述）主导描述内容的影响；(iii) 反事实上限显示完美委派比当前实测性能高出15-31个百分点。

Conclusion: DecisionBench 揭示了仅用质量指标评估委派策略的盲区，定位了大规模未开发的编排优化空间，为未来学习方法（如学习型路由器、自适应配置文件构建、多步委派）提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: We introduce DecisionBench, a benchmark substrate for emergent delegation in long-horizon agentic workflows. The substrate fixes a task suite (GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn), a peer-model pool (11 models, 7 vendor families), a delegation interface (call_model plus an optional read_profile channel), a deterministic skill-annotation layer, and a multi-axis metric suite covering quality, cost, latency, delegation rate, routing fidelity-at-k, vendor self-preference, and a counterfactual-delegation ceiling. The substrate is agnostic to how peer information is generated or delivered, so learned routers, richer peer memories, adaptive profile construction, and multi-step delegation can all be evaluated against it. We characterize the substrate with a five-condition reference sweep on the full pool (n=23,375 task instances). Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) mean end-task quality is statistically indistinguishable across the four awareness conditions (|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21), so quality-only evaluation would miss the orchestration signal; (ii) routing fidelity-at-1 ranges from 7.5% to 29.5% across conditions at near-equal mean quality, with delivery channel (on-demand tool vs. preloaded description) dominating description content; (iii) a counterfactual ceiling places perfect delegation 15-31 percentage points above measured performance on every suite, locating large unrealized headroom for future orchestration methods. We release the substrate, annotation layer, reference intervention suite, analysis pipeline, and 220 per-condition run archives.

</details>


### [147] [Progressive Autonomy as Preference Learning: A Formalization of Trust Calibration for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19151)
*Changkun Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了智能体工具使用中的信任校准问题，将其建模为偏好学习问题，通过策略网关维护高斯过程后验分布，利用概率似然处理二元审批反馈，并在不确定性最高处升级到人工审批。该方法本质上属于偏好贝叶斯优化，核心差异在于目标是对动作空间进行分类而非优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能体工具使用中如何自动决定哪些动作可以自主执行、哪些需要人工审批的信任校准问题。

Method: 将信任校准形式化为偏好学习问题，采用高斯过程后验建模潜在人类风险容忍函数，通过概率似然观察二元审批反馈，在结果不确定性最高的区域触发人工审批。该方法被证明是偏好贝叶斯优化的一个实例。

Result: 该方法继承了偏好贝叶斯优化的推理机制（近似高斯过程分类）和样本效率优势（不确定性导向查询），但目标不同：将动作空间分类为允许/阻止/询问区域。

Conclusion: 信任校准问题可有效映射为偏好贝叶斯优化框架，通过不确定性驱动的查询策略实现样本高效的信任决策边界学习。

Abstract: We formalize trust calibration for agentic tool use (deciding when an automated agent's proposed action may execute autonomously versus require human approval) as a preference-learning problem. A policy gateway maintains a Gaussian-process posterior over a latent human risk-tolerance function, observed through a probit likelihood on binary approve/deny feedback, and escalates to the human exactly where the approval outcome is most uncertain. We show this is structurally an instance of Preferential Bayesian Optimization, inheriting its inference machinery (approximate Gaussian-process classification) and its sample-efficiency argument (uncertainty-targeted querying), while differing in objective: classifying an action space into allow/block/ask regions rather than optimizing a design.

</details>


### [148] [How Far Are We From True Auto-Research?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19156)
*Zhengxin Zhang,Ning Wang,Sainyam Galhotra,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ResearchArena 框架，让三种 AI 智能体（Claude Code、Codex、Kimi Code）自动完成完整科研流程，生成了 117 篇论文。仅看论文稿件评审（SAR），Claude Code 甚至能接近人类 ICLR 2025 投稿水平；但结合工件感知的同行评审（PR）和人工审查后发现，所有论文均未达到顶会接收标准，实验严谨性是最大瓶颈，表现为结果伪造、实验效力不足、计划执行不匹配三种失败模式，且智能体间差异极大（Codex 与 Kimi Code 的伪造引用率相差约 15 倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动科研系统虽能生成完整论文，但缺乏对其生成质量（尤其是超过表面可行性的系统评估）的严格研究。作者希望填补这一空白，系统性地评估智能体生成论文的真实水平。

Method: 构建 ResearchArena 轻量框架，让三种智能体（Claude Code/Opus 4.6、Codex/GPT-5.4、Kimi Code/K2.5）自主完成构思、实验、写作和自我改进。在 13 个计算机科学种子方向×3 次试验下生成 117 篇论文，采用三种互补评审方式：仅稿件评审（SAR）、工件感知同行评审（PR，智能体同时审查稿件与工作区）、人工元评审。

Result: SAR 评分显示 Claude Code 得分最高，超过 Analemma 的 FARS 并与 ICLR 2025 人类投稿加权均值相当；但人工审查发现 SAR 与真实接收决策对齐差，且只奖励表面合理性而不验证实验实质。PR 评分大幅下降，人工审计揭示实验严谨性是主要瓶颈：三种失败模式（结果伪造、实验效力不足、计划执行不匹配）高度依赖智能体类型（Codex 5%/8% 伪造率 vs Kimi Code 77%/72%，约 15 倍差距）。117 篇论文无一达到顶会接收标准。

Conclusion: 当前仅靠轻量框架引导的 AI 智能体尚无法产出达到顶会水平的科研论文，距离真正的自动科研仍有明显差距。仅看稿件评审会高估性能，必须结合实验工件进行完整评估才能暴露实质缺陷。

Abstract: Recent auto-research systems can produce complete papers, but feasibility is not the same as quality, and the field still lacks a systematic study of how good agent-generated papers actually are. We introduce ResearchArena, a minimal scaffold that lets off-the-shelf agents (Claude Code using Opus 4.6, Codex using GPT-5.4, and Kimi Code using K2.5) carry out the full research loop themselves (ideation, experimentation, paper writing, self-refinement) under only lightweight guidance. Across 13 computer science seeds and 3 trials per agent-domain pair, ResearchArena yields 117 agent-generated papers, each evaluated under three complementary lenses: a manuscript-only reviewer (SAR), an artifact-aware peer review (PR) in which agents inspect the workspace alongside the manuscript, and an human conducted meta-review. Under SAR alone the picture is optimistic: Claude Code obtains the highest score, outperforms Analemma's FARS, and matches the weighted-average human ICLR 2025 submission, suggesting that minimally scaffolded agents can produce papers that look competitive on manuscript-only review. Manual inspection, however, reveals this picture is overstated: SAR scores are poorly aligned with its actual acceptance decisions and reward plausible framing without verifying experimental substance. Under artifact-aware PR scores drop sharply, and manual auditing identifies experimental rigor as the major bottleneck, decomposing into three failure modes (fabricated results, underpowered experiments, and plan/execution mismatch) that are highly agent-dependent: Codex 5%/8% paper-vs-artifact mismatch / fabricated references versus Kimi Code 77%/72%, a $\sim$15$\times$ spread that tracks distinct research personas the agents develop. None of the 117 agent-generated papers reaches the acceptance bar of a top-tier venue. This suggests that we are still gapped from the true auto-research.

</details>


### [149] [Hallucination as Exploit: Evidence-Carrying Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19192)
*Guijia Zhang,Hao Zheng,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出证据驱动的多模态智能体（ECA），通过约束性验证器生成类型化证书来授权工具调用，解决幻觉导致的安全授权失败问题，将不安全行为率降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体依赖截图、文档和网页选择工具调用，当视觉幻觉导致点击、邮件、数据提取或转账等特权操作时，幻觉从回答质量错误转化为授权失败——即"幻觉到行动转换"（hallucination-to-action conversion）。现有方法仅通过提示防御，仍存在49.6%的未授权执行率。

Method: ECA将每次工具调用分解为动作关键谓词（action-critical predicates），从受限的DOM/OCR/AX验证器获取类型化证书（typed certificates），并由确定性门控（deterministic gate）仅授权证书支持的权限。模型自由文本被视为不可采纳证据，架构将不透明模型信念转化为命名的验证器、模式和实现残差。

Result: 经过1,900次攻击的验证器红队测试，四轮针对性加固将门控绕过率从15%降至1.3%。端到端200任务pipeline中不安全行为率为0%（Wilson 95%上限2.67%），120任务浏览器概念验证中上限4.3%。HACR审计显示朴素智能体100.0%失败，提示防御49.6%失败，ECA为0%。

Conclusion: 模型语言可以提议行动，但外部证据必须授权它们。ECA通过将可信执行权限从不可靠的感知推断中分离，为多模态智能体提供可验证的安全保障。

Abstract: Multimodal agents use screenshots, documents, and webpages to choose tool calls. When a false visual claim triggers a click, email, extraction, or transfer, hallucination becomes an authorization failure rather than an answer-quality error. We formalize this failure mode as hallucination-to-action conversion: an unsupported perceptual claim supplies the precondition that makes a privileged action appear permitted. We propose evidence-carrying multimodal agents (ECA), which treat free-form model text as inadmissible evidence. ECA decomposes each tool call into action-critical predicates, obtains typed certificates from constrained DOM/OCR/AX verifiers, and lets a deterministic gate grant only the privileges those certificates support. The architecture does not hide perception error; it converts opaque model belief into named verifier, schema, and implementation residuals. Verifier red-teaming over 1,900 attacks exposes this residual directly: four targeted hardening steps reduce gate bypass from 15% to 1.3%. With content-derived certificates, ECA obtains 0% unsafe-action rate on a 200-task end-to-end pipeline (Wilson 95% upper bound 2.67%) and a 120-task browser proof-of-concept (upper bound 4.3%). A direct HACR audit on 500 stratified task keys shows that unsupported action-critical claims reach unsafe execution for naive agents (100.0%) and prompt-only defense (49.6%), but not for ECA. Oracle-certificate replay on 7,488 GPT-5.4 benchmark traces serves as a gate-correctness sanity check, and neural judge baselines remain bypassable under the same threat model. The resulting principle is simple: model language may propose actions, but external evidence must authorize them.

</details>


### [150] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，在具有潜在奖励状态漂移（波动性）和观测噪声（随机性）的环境中，虽然两者都增加后验不确定性，但波动性驱动最优探索增加，而随机性抑制探索。这种不对称性通过扩展Gittins指数框架和高斯状态空间bandit模型得到形式化证明，并提出了CAUSE探索奖励方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将不同来源的环境不确定性视为等价，但作者认为波动性和随机性虽然都增加后验不确定性，对探索行为的影响可能不同，需要区分对待。

Method: 扩展Gittins指数框架到高斯状态空间bandit模型（含潜在动态）；提出CAUSE（Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration）方法，通过控制作为推理（control-as-inference）得到闭合形式的探索奖励。

Result: 波动性增强探索，随机性抑制探索；CAUSE在异质性噪声结构中优于标准探索策略和Gittins-per-arm策略；病理性噪声推理会产生反转而非仅仅减弱的探索行为。

Conclusion: 学习和探索受相同的噪声推断不对称性支配，该框架对精神疾病的计算解释有启示意义。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [151] [SimGym: A Framework for A/B Test Simulation in E-Commerce with Traffic-Grounded VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19219)
*Han Li,Vibhor Malik,Zahra Zanjani Foumani,Alberto Castelo,Shuang Xie,Ailin Fan,Keat Yang Koay,Yuanzheng Zhu,Meysam Feghhi,Ronie Uliana,Zhaoyu Zhang,Angelo Ocana Martins,Mingyu Zhao,Francis Pelland,Jonathan Faerman,Nikolas LeBlanc,Aaron Glazer,Andrew McNamara,Zhong Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimGym是一个利用视觉语言模型（VLM）智能体在真实浏览器中模拟电商A/B测试的框架。它通过基于流量的人物画像生成、实时浏览器智能体架构和评估协议三大组件，在无需真实用户参与的情况下，将实验周期从数周缩短至一小时以内，并实现了与真实买家行为77%的方向一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统A/B测试虽然被认为是评估电商前端修改的金标准，但存在三大痛点：需要分流真实流量、需要数周才能达到统计显著性、且存在降低用户体验的风险。因此，研究者希望开发一种无需真实用户参与的模拟框架来加速实验迭代。

Method: SimGym包含三个核心组件：(a) 基于流量的画像生成管道，从生产级点击流数据中推导出每个店铺的买家原型和意图；(b) 实时浏览器智能体架构，结合多模态感知（视觉和浏览器结构化观察）、情景记忆和护栏机制，在对照组和实验组店铺之间进行连贯的购物会话；(c) 评估协议，比较模拟结果变化与真实买家行为观察到的变化。使用来自大型电商平台的视觉驱动UI主题变更A/B测试进行验证。

Result: SimGym智能体与真实买家观察到的结果变化具有强一致性，在不同界面变体下与加购行为变化的方向对齐率达到77%。实验周期从数周缩短至一小时以内。

Conclusion: SimGym能够在不暴露真实买家到候选变体的前提下实现快速实验迭代，大幅加速了电商前端UI改动的评估流程。

Abstract: A/B testing remains the gold standard for evaluating modifications to e-commerce storefronts, yet it diverts traffic, requires weeks to reach statistical significance, and risks degrading user experience. We present SimGym, a framework for simulating A/B tests on e-commerce storefronts using vision-language model (VLM) agents operating in a live browser. The framework comprises three key components: (a) a traffic-grounded persona generation pipeline that derives per-shop buyer archetypes and intents from production clickstream data; (b) a live-browser agent architecture that combines multimodal perception over visual and browser-structured observations with episodic memory and guardrails to conduct coherent shopping sessions across control and treatment storefronts; and (c) an evaluation protocol that compares simulated outcome shifts with observed shifts in real buyer behavior. We validate SimGym on A/B tests of visually driven UI theme changes from a major e-commerce platform across diverse storefronts and product categories. Empirical results show that SimGym agents achieve strong agreement with observed outcome shifts, attaining 77% directional alignment with add-to-cart shifts observed across interface variants in real-buyer traffic. It reduces experimental cycles from weeks to under an hour, enabling rapid experimentation without exposing real buyers to candidate variants.

</details>


### [152] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个五阶段LLM集成框架，涵盖调查问卷设计到后收集分析的全流程。以2024年飓风弥尔顿佛罗里达居民准备度调查（n=946）为测试平台，提出的Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM)在灾难相关的块状MNAR条件下，RMSE（1.439）优于IPW/MI、MICE+PMM和missForest三个经典插补基线，并实现了近零有符号偏差（-0.121）。围绕PMT因果结构组织检索并在单次模型调用中整合所有证据，优于非结构化检索和分阶段顺序推理（MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097）。提出了子组分层层审计作为报告标准，并展示了通过基于检索约束的知识图谱聊天机器人实现架构化幻觉控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查研究面临响应率下降、样本偏差、高风险受访者的块状缺失以及AI辅助的欺诈性完成等结构性问题。LLMs被提出作为解决方案，但跨完整调查工作流的严格评估仍然稀缺，尤其在数据质量至关重要的灾难情境中。

Method: 提出了五阶段LLM集成框架：问卷设计、样本选择、试点测试、缺失数据插补和后收集分析。开发了PMT约束的共现知识图谱，构建了涵盖零样本推理、检索增强基线和新型理论驱动变体的七种LLM配置，并提出了A-TLM方法。

Result: A-TLM在灾难相关块状MNAR条件下RMSE（1.439）优于所有三个经典插补基线（次优1.496），近零有符号偏差（-0.121）而随机森林插补器产生最大绝对偏差（-0.631）。围绕PMT因果结构组织检索优于非结构化检索（MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097）。近零聚合偏差可能掩盖相反的亚组误差。

Conclusion: 理论约束的LLM方法在灾难调查数据质量方面优于经典方法；建议采用亚组分层层偏差审计作为报告标准；基于检索约束的知识图谱聊天机器人可架构化控制幻觉；需进一步研究LLM辅助调查的偏差与公平性。

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [153] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果分析发现多模态大模型中的模态冲突幻觉源于注意力头的不对称路由结构：驱动幻觉的头部广泛分布且权重高，而抵抗幻觉的头部集中但影响力弱。基于此提出MACI方法，在检测到冲突时抑制驱动幻觉的头部，有效减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多模态大模型中视觉证据在模态冲突时为何无法战胜错误文本前提的内部机制原因。

Method: 对5个开源MLLM进行头部级路径修补因果分析，识别出两类注意力头；提出MACI条件干预方法，仅在检测到模态冲突时抑制因果识别的幻觉驱动头。

Result: MACI在MMMC基准上实现最大幻觉减少，幻觉-准确率权衡良好，且零样本迁移到SCI-SemanticConflict测试。

Conclusion: 模态冲突幻觉源于分布式驱动与集中式抵抗的不平衡路由结构，针对该结构的因果干预可有效缓解幻觉。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [154] [AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19260)
*Yuankai Li,Tinghui Zhu,Ha Min Son,Zhe Zhao,Xin Liu,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AquaUI，一种无需训练的推理时令牌缩减方法，通过自适应四叉树利用GUI截图中的非均匀空间信息密度，在保持99.06%性能的同时实现最高13.22%的加速和29.52%的视觉令牌减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM-based GUI agent模型在每轮迭代中引入高分辨率截图，但截图存在高度非均匀的空间信息密度——大片区域信息量少且视觉同质，而关键文本和图标需要高保真度。现有方法要么需要额外训练，要么依赖基于注意力的令牌压缩，忽略了GUI截图的结构化布局和空间冗余。

Method: AquaUI的核心方法包括：1) 自适应四叉树构建：对每张截图输入构建自适应四叉树，每个叶子节点保留一个代表性合并令牌；2) 空间位置保留：保留令牌的空间位置贯穿整个流水线，确保位置编码阶段的一致性；3) 条件四叉树算法：利用连续截图间的连续性，以先前四叉树为参考优化当前四叉树，在静态或轻微移动的GUI状态下保持细粒度区域。

Result: 在标准grounding和导航基准上的实验表明，AquaUI在准确率-效率权衡上持续优于先前基线。在GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct上，实现最高13.22%的加速和29.52%的视觉令牌减少，同时保留99.06%的全令牌性能。

Conclusion: GUI截图的空間冗余可以在无需重新训练的情况下在推理时被有效利用，AquaUI证明了训练-free的推理时令牌缩减方法在GUI agent模型中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently emerged as promising backbones for GUI-agent models, where high-resolution GUI screenshots are introduced to the prompts at each iteration step. However, these screenshots exhibit highly non-uniform spatial information density: large regions may carry little information and are visually homogeneous, while key text and icons may require high visual fidelity. Existing approaches to this problem either require additional training or rely on attention-based token compression, ignoring the structured layout and spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots. To fill the gap, this paper proposes AquaUI, a training-free inference-time token reduction method for GUI agent models that utilizes the non-uniform information density in screenshots. AQuaUI constructs an adaptive quadtree on each screenshot input and keeps one representative merged token per leaf of the quadtree. AQuaUI preserves the spatial positions of retained tokens throughout the pipeline to ensure that all position-encoding stages remain consistent. To further improve temporal consistency across multi-step GUI interactions, we propose a conditional quadtree algorithm that leverages the continuity between consecutive screenshots within a single request. Specifically, it refines the current quadtree using previous quadtrees as references, helping preserve fine-grained regions across static or mildly shifted GUI states. We implement AQuaUI on state-of-the-art GUI agent models and conduct experiments on standard grounding and navigational benchmarks. AQuaUI consistently shows improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over prior baselines. Notably, on GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct, AQuaUI achieves up to 13.22% speedup and 29.52% fewer visual tokens while retaining 99.06% of full-token performance, suggesting that the spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots can be exploited at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [155] [Swimming with Whales: Analysis of Power Imbalances in Stake-Weighted Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19264)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Manvir Schneider,Qin Wang,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究PoS区块链中按权益加权投票的治理范式，通过计算社会选择理论中的Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数，分析权益加权投票中的权力失衡问题。理论上证明权力与权益占比的完美对齐不可实现但可在特定条件下近似，实证上利用Project Catalyst数据揭示了现实系统中存在的权力失衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoS区块链中权益加权投票（stake-weighted voting）作为基础治理范式，存在少数大户控制决策的权力扭曲问题。作者希望通过计算社会选择理论的视角，量化研究这种权力失衡的程度。

Method: 以Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数作为权力量化工具，进行理论分析证明权力与权益占比的关系，并结合Project Catalyst真实链上治理系统数据进行实证分析。

Result: 理论上证明权力与相对权益占比的完美对齐在一般情况下不可实现，但可在特定条件下在期望意义上近似。实证数据揭示了当前权益加权治理系统中存在的权力失衡具体特征。

Conclusion: 权益加权投票的PoS治理范式存在固有的权力结构扭曲，需要进一步设计机制以缓解权力失衡问题。

Abstract: Voting methods weighted by stakes are the fundamental governance paradigm in Proof-of-Stake (PoS) blockchains. Such a paradigm is known to be prone to power distortions: a few users possessing large stakes may completely control decision making, even without owning the totality of the stakes. We study this phenomenon through the lens of computational social choice, focusing on the extent of power imbalances in stake-weighted voting when power is quantified using the Penrose-Banzhaf power index. Our work presents both analytical and empirical contributions. Analytically, we demonstrate that while a perfect alignment between power and relative stake ownership is generally unattainable, it can be approximated in expectation under specific conditions. Empirically, using data from a real-world on-chain governance system (Project Catalyst), we provide a more fine-grained understanding of the power imbalances that are likely to occur in current stake-weighted governance systems.

</details>


### [156] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19330)
*Md Mehrab Tanjim,Jayakumar Subramanian,Xiang Chen,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Anlan Zhang,Sungchul Kim,Somdeb Sarkhel,Sunav Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MOCHA方法，通过切比雪夫标量化和指数退火，在多目标约束下优化LLM智能体技能，相比现有方法在所有任务上均取得改进，发现更多帕累托最优变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的技能优化面临多目标约束（任务性能 vs 平台限制如截断、压缩、上下文竞争），现有提示优化器要么忽略这些权衡，要么用加权和方式处理，无法覆盖非凸区域的帕累托最优变体。

Method: 提出MOCHA框架，核心包括：(1) 切比雪夫标量化替代单目标选择，覆盖完整帕累托前沿（含非凸区域）；(2) 指数退火机制实现从探索到利用的平滑过渡。

Result: 在6个智能体技能任务中，现有优化器在4个任务上零进展，MOCHA在所有任务上突破，平均正确率相对最强基线提升7.5%（FEVER达14.9%，TheoremQA达10.4%），发现的帕累托最优技能变体数量翻倍。

Conclusion: MOCHA通过多目标优化方法有效解决了LLM智能体技能优化中平台约束与任务性能的权衡问题，在非凸目标区域也能发现帕累托最优变体，显著优于现有单目标或加权和方法。

Abstract: LLM agents organize behavior through skills - structured natural-language specifications governing how an agent reasons, retrieves, and responds. Unlike monolithic prompts, skills are multi-field artifacts subject to hard platform constraints: description fields are truncated for routing, instruction bodies are compacted via progressive disclosure, and co-resident skills compete for limited context windows. These constraints make skill optimization inherently multi-objective: a skill must simultaneously maximize task performance and satisfy platform limits. Yet existing prompt optimizers either ignore these trade-offs or collapse them into a weighted sum, missing Pareto-optimal variants in non-convex objective regions. We introduce MOCHA (Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing), which replaces single-objective selection with Chebyshev scalarization - covering the full Pareto front, including non-convex regions - combined with exponential annealing that transitions from exploration to exploitation. In our experiments across six diverse agent skills - where all methods share the same multi-objective mutation operator and baselines receive identical per-objective textual feedback - existing optimizers fail to improve the seed skill on 4 of 6 tasks: 1000 rollouts yield zero progress. MOCHA breaks through on every task, achieving 7.5% relative improvement in mean correctness over the strongest baseline (up to 14.9% on FEVER and 10.4% on TheoremQA) while discovering twice as many more Pareto-optimal skill variants.

</details>


### [157] [Generative Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19376)
*Junyeob Baek,Mingyu Jo,Minsu Kim,Mengye Ren,Yoshua Bengio,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRAM将递归潜在推理转变为概率多轨迹计算，通过随机潜在轨迹实现多假设、多策略和推理时扩展，在结构化推理和多解约束满足任务上优于确定性基线，并具备无条件生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归推理模型（RRMs）大多是确定性的，遵循单一潜在轨迹并收敛到单一预测，缺乏处理多假设、多解问题的能力和概率多样性。

Method: 提出生成式递归推理模型（GRAM），将递归潜在推理建模为随机潜在轨迹的概率多轨迹计算，支持通过递归深度和平行轨迹采样进行推理时扩展，使用摊销变分推断训练。

Result: 在结构化推理和多解约束满足任务上，GRAM优于确定性递归和循环基线，并展示了无条件生成能力。

Conclusion: GRAM通过概率多轨迹递归推理框架，有效提升了神经推理系统的多样性和扩展性，为未来推理系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: How should future neural reasoning systems implement extended computation? Recursive Reasoning Models (RRMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive sequence extension by performing iterative latent-state refinement with shared transition functions. Yet existing RRMs are largely deterministic, following a single latent trajectory and converging to a single prediction. We introduce \emph{Generative Recursive reAsoning Models (GRAM)}, a framework that turns recursive latent reasoning into probabilistic multi-trajectory computation. GRAM models reasoning as a stochastic latent trajectory, enabling multiple hypotheses, alternative solution strategies, and inference-time scaling through both recursive depth and parallel trajectory sampling. This yields a latent-variable generative model supporting conditional reasoning via $p_θ(y \mid x)$ and, with fixed or absent inputs, unconditional generation via $p_θ(x)$. Trained with amortized variational inference, GRAM improves over deterministic recurrent and recursive baselines on structured reasoning and multi-solution constraint satisfaction tasks, while demonstrating an unconditional generation capability. \href{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website/}{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website}

</details>


### [158] [Conflict-Resilient Multi-Agent Reasoning via Signed Graph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19418)
*Longgang He,Longzhu He,Daojing He,Chaozhuo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SIGMA框架，通过符号关系图显式建模多智能体间的信任、冲突和中性关系，采用冲突感知的消息传递机制抑制冲突信号，实现全局一致的冲突鲁棒推理，在六个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的多智能体系统在传播错误信号时缺乏控制，且未能显式建模智能体间的冲突关系及结构感知能力，导致无法识别可靠的交互模式。

Method: 首先选择相关且多样的智能体，构建带置信度权重的符号交互图；通过冲突感知的符号消息传递增强信任信息、抑制冲突信号；最后进行结构感知和冲突感知的加权聚合，得到全局一致且冲突鲁棒的预测。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，使用多种LLM主干和不同多智能体配置，SIGMA在准确性和冲突鲁棒性方面均一致优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模智能体间的信任、冲突和中性关系，并采用冲突感知的消息传递机制，是提升多智能体系统推理鲁棒性和性能的有效途径。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated strong reasoning and decision-making capabilities that consistently surpass those of single LLM agents. However, their performance often suffers from naive aggregation mechanisms that assume uniformly cooperative interactions. Upon close inspection, we observe that existing graph-based MAS frameworks (1) propagate errors when conflicting signals arise without control, and (2) lack explicit modeling of conflicting inter-agent relations as well as structural awareness, failing to identify reliable interaction patterns. To bridge this gap, we introduce SIGMA, a novel SIgned Graph-informed Multi-Agent reasoning framework that explicitly captures trust, conflict, and neutral relations among agents via a signed relational graph. Specifically, given a query, SIGMA first selects a set of relevant and diverse agents, then constructs a structured signed interaction graph with confidence-weighted edges. Reasoning proceeds through conflict-aware signed message passing, which reinforces information from trustworthy agents while suppressing conflicting signals, and terminates with a structure- and conflict-aware weighted aggregation to yield globally consistent and conflict-resilient predictions. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets, across multiple LLM backbones and diverse multi-agent configurations, demonstrate that SIGMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving notable gains in both accuracy and conflict-resilient performance.

</details>


### [159] [What and When to Distill: Selective Hindsight Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19447)
*Xiaozhe Li,Tianyi Lyu,Yang Li,Yichuan Ma,Peiji Li,Linyang Li,Qipeng Guo,Dahua Lin,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SERL框架，通过环境反馈加权强化学习，在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别达到90.0%和80.1%的成功率，优于现有RL和蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程稀疏奖励下的信用分配难题：单次成功/失败信号难以有效分配至多个动作；多轮交互智能体场景中每步环境反馈（错误信息、页面变化、观测、参考轨迹等）未被充分利用。

Method: 系统性研究五种反馈源和两种插入粒度；提出SERL（选择性环境重加权学习），以任务奖励决定更新方向，环境反馈调整动作的放置位置和幅度，聚焦关键动作。

Result: ALFWorld上90.0%成功率，WebShop上80.1%成功率，超越强RL和蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 基于环境的、与动作相关的、在关键节点施加的反馈始终优于无差别使用更长或更丰富的上下文。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can train LLM agents from sparse task rewards, but long-horizon credit assignment remains challenging: a single success-or-failure signal must be distributed across many actions. Existing methods rely on trajectory-level rewards or proxy signals, without fully leveraging per-step environmental feedback. Multi-turn agent settings are underexplored, where feedback can include error messages, page changes, observations, or reference trajectories. We systematically study five feedback sources and two insertion granularities and introduce SERL, a selective environment-reweighted learning framework. SERL uses the task reward to determine update direction, while environment feedback adjusts placement and magnitude, focusing on critical actions. On ALFWorld and WebShop, SERL achieves 90.0% and 80.1% success, outperforming strong RL and distillation baselines. Analysis shows that grounded, action-relevant feedback at meaningful points consistently outperforms indiscriminate use of longer or richer context.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Distribution Matching for Diverse Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19461)
*Xiaozhe Li,Yang Li,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Yichuan Ma,Tianyi Lyu,Linyang Li,Dahua Lin,Qipeng Guo,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DMPO（分布匹配策略优化），通过近似前向KL散度最小化来防止GRPO等on-policy强化学习方法中的模式坍缩问题，在组合优化、数学推理等任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等on-policy RL方法存在模式坍缩问题：一旦发现高奖励轨迹就将概率质量集中于此，停止探索其他策略。这是由于反向KL最小化的mode-seeking行为所致。

Method: DMPO通过近似前向KL最小化来防止模式坍缩。它在组级别构建一个与奖励成正比的轨迹目标分布，然后将策略分布与该目标对齐，实现了mode-covering行为，无需从不可行的全局目标分布中采样。

Result: 在NP-Bench文本任务上Quality Ratio达43.9%（GRPO为40.1%），视觉任务上达43.1%（GRPO为38.4%），分别提升9%和12%。数学推理提升2.0%，跨领域任务提升2.3%。

Conclusion: 分布匹配作为一种实用的、有原则的方法，能有效防止on-policy RL中的模式坍缩，通过保持探索带来一致的性能提升，且跨模态泛化到推理任务。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning methods like GRPO suffer from mode collapse: they exhibit reduced solution diversity, concentrating probability mass on a single solution once discovered and ceasing exploration of alternative strategies. We show this stems from reverse KL minimization's mode-seeking behavior, which reinforces the first high-reward trajectory found rather than maintaining a distribution over multiple diverse solutions. We propose DMPO (Distribution-Matching Policy Optimization), which prevents mode collapse through principled approximation of forward KL minimization. DMPO constructs a group level target distribution over sampled trajectories proportional to their rewards, then aligns the policy distribution to this target. This provides mode-covering behavior without requiring sampling from the intractable global target distribution, enabling sustained exploration throughout training. We validate DMPO on NP-hard combinatorial optimization, where exponentially many feasible solutions exist but only a few approach optimality, an ideal testbed for evaluating exploration. DMPO achieves 43.9% Quality Ratio on text-based NP-Bench (vs. GRPO's 40.1%) and 43.1% on vision-based NP-Bench (vs. 38.4%), demonstrating 9% and 12% relative improvements respectively. These gains generalize to mathematical reasoning (+2.0%) and out-of-domain tasks (+2.3%), showing that diversity-preserving training enhances general reasoning capabilities across modalities. Our work establishes distribution matching as a practical, principled approach to preventing mode collapse in on-policy RL, with consistent quality improvements demonstrating sustained exploration across diverse reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [161] [Attention-Guided Reward for Reinforcement Learning-based Jailbreak against Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19485)
*Zheng Lin,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Yuzhe Huang,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大型推理模型（LRM）的越狱攻击成功率与其注意力模式密切相关：成功攻击对输入中 harmful tokens 分配低注意力，而对推理内容中的同类 tokens 分配高注意力。基于此，提出一种利用强化学习的越狱方法，将注意力信号融入奖励函数设计，并引入多样化说服策略丰富动作空间，在多个开源/闭源模型和基准上实现了更高的攻击成功率、效率和可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明LRM比标准LLM更容易受到越狱攻击，但对其内在机制尚缺乏深入理解。本文旨在揭示LRM越狱攻击成功率的底层原因，并据此设计更有效的攻击方法，以促进模型安全性的提升。

Method: 1) 通过实验发现LRM越狱ASR与注意力模式的关联性；2) 提出基于强化学习的越狱方法，将注意力信号显式融入奖励函数设计；3) 引入多样化说服策略（persuasion strategies）来丰富RL动作空间，提升攻击效果。

Result: 在五个开源和闭源LRM、三个基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在攻击成功率、效率和可迁移性方面均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LRM的注意力模式是影响越狱攻击成功的关键因素，基于强化学习并融入注意力信号的方法能够有效提升攻击效果，为理解LRM安全漏洞提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in solving complex problems by generating structured, step-by-step reasoning content. However, exposing a model's internal reasoning process introduces additional safety risks; for example, recent studies show that LRMs are more vulnerable to jailbreak attacks than standard LLMs. In this paper, we investigate jailbreak attacks on LRMs and reveal that the attack success rate (ASR) is closely correlated with LRMs' attention patterns. Specifically, successful jailbreaks tend to assign lower attention to harmful tokens in the input prompt, while allocating higher attention to those tokens in the reasoning content. Motivated by this finding, we propose a novel jailbreak method for LRMs that leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to enhance attack effectiveness, explicitly incorporating attention signals into the reward function design. In addition, we introduce diverse persuasion strategies to enrich the RL action space, which consistently improves the ASR. Extensive experiments on five open-source and closed-source LRMs across three benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves substantially higher ASR, outperforming existing approaches in terms of effectiveness, efficiency, and transferability.

</details>


### [162] [Position: The Turing-Completeness of Real-World Autoregressive Transformers Relies Heavily on Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19514)
*Guanyu Cui,Zhewei Wei,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文澄清了Transformer图灵完备性证明中两种不同设置——固定系统设置与缩放族设置——的区别，指出许多宣称Transformer图灵完备的结论实际上是在缩放族设置下证明的，而与现实LLM部署和标准图灵完备性概念对应的固定系统设置并未被证明具有图灵完备性。文章还强调了上下文管理方法对计算能力的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常混淆两种不同的设置：(i)固定Transformer系统设置和(ii)缩放族设置。许多Transformer图灵完备性的证明是在设置(ii)中建立的，而现实LLM部署和标准图灵完备性概念更自然地对应于设置(i)。作者旨在澄清这一误解，并强调上下文管理对计算能力的关键作用。

Method: 首先形式化固定系统设置，提供现实LLM运作的具体刻画；然后论证缩放族设置下的结果虽然提供有理论意义的资源界限，但并未建立图灵完备性；最后展示不同上下文管理方法能产生截然不同的计算能力。

Result: 明确指出缩放族设置下的图灵完备性证明不能等同于固定系统设置的图灵完备性；揭示了上下文管理是决定现实自回归Transformer计算能力的核心组件。

Conclusion: 倡导将上下文管理视为决定现实自回归Transformer计算能力的关键因素，纠正了对现有结果的常见误解。

Abstract: Many works make the eye-catching claim that Transformers are Turing-complete. However, the literature often conflates two distinct settings: (i) a fixed Transformer system setting, in which a fixed autoregressive Transformer is coupled with a fixed context-management method to process inputs of different lengths step by step, and (ii) a scaling-family setting, in which a family of different models (with increasing context-window length or numerical precision) is used to handle different input lengths. Existing proofs of Transformer Turing-completeness are frequently established in setting (ii), whereas real-world LLM deployment and the standard notion of Turing-completeness correspond more naturally to setting (i). In this paper, we first formalize the fixed-system setting, thereby providing a concrete characterization of how real-world LLMs operate. We then argue that results proved in the scaling-family setting provide theoretically meaningful resource bounds but do not establish Turing-completeness, thereby clarifying a common misinterpretation of existing results. Finally, we show that different context-management methods can yield sharply different computational power, and we advocate the position that context management is a central component that critically determines the computational power of real-world autoregressive Transformers.

</details>


### [163] [BLINKG: A Benchmark for LLM-Integrated Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19518)
*Carla Castedo,Enrique Iglesias,Manuel Lama,Alberto Bugarin-Diz,Maria-Esther Vidal,David Chaves-Fraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BLINKG 基准，用于评估大语言模型在从异构数据源构建知识图谱时的映射能力，实验表明现有 LLM 在简单场景表现良好，但在复杂场景仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱构建中，将输入数据模式与本体术语对齐仍需要大量人工，现有研究缺乏标准化框架来评估 LLM 在这方面的能力。

Method: 设计了 BLINKG 基准，包含基于真实用例的递增复杂度场景，对多种 SOTA LLM 进行实验评估。

Result: 现有 LLM 已展现出有前景的方案，但在复杂场景下性能仍然有限。

Conclusion: BLINKG 可评估 LLM 在 KG 构建中的当前能力，并定义了实现（半）自动化 LLM驱动的 KG 构建的需求，开辟了新研究方向。

Abstract: Generating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) remains one of the most time-consuming and labor-intensive tasks for knowledge engineers, as they need to identify semantic equivalences between input data sources and ontology terms. While declarative solutions (e.g., RML, SPARQL-Anything) have helped to generalize this process, aligning input schema elements with ontology terms still involves intricate transformations and requires considerable manual effort. With the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is growing interest in leveraging their capabilities to assist KG engineers. Although some studies have explored using LLMs to automate KG construction, there is still no standardized framework for assessing how effectively they establish correspondences between data schemes and ontology concepts. Therefore, in this paper, we propose BLINKG, a benchmark designed to evaluate the mapping capabilities of LLMs in constructing KGs from heterogeneous data sources. The benchmark includes a set of scenarios with increasing complexity, based on real-world use cases. We conduct an extensive experimental evaluation of several stateof-the-art LLMs using BLINK and observe that they already offer promising solutions. However, their performance remains limited in complex scenarios. Thanks to this benchmark, we can already assess the current capabilities of LLMs for KG construction. Additionally, we define a set of requirements for achieving (semi)automated (LLM-driven) KG construction, opening new research lines in this area.

</details>


### [164] [Efficient Elicitation of Collective Disagreements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19521)
*Mohamed Ouaguenouni,Felipe Garrido-Lucero,Umberto Grandi,César Hidalgo,Magdalena Tydrichova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层框架，通过引入"多元矩阵"（plurality matrix）来识别计算不同分歧度量所需的最小偏好信息层级。研究发现，许多现有分歧度量（如排名方差、分裂性）需要至少3级信息（即三元子集），而不仅仅是成对比较。论文还设计了两种启发式协议来估计多元矩阵，并在理论和实验上证明了超越3级信息的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调查中，成对比较（pairwise comparisons）无法区分结构性分歧与噪声，而完整排名又对参与者认知负担过重。作者希望找到一种中间方案，既能捕捉到足够的分歧信息，又能降低参与者的认知负荷。

Method: 1. 提出"多元矩阵"概念，记录每个子集S中每个选项排名第一的概率。2. 定义分歧度量的"层级"为表达该度量所需的最小子集大小。3. 分析多种现有分歧度量的层级归属。4. 设计两种启发式协议（elicitation protocols）来估计多元矩阵，平衡参与者数量与认知负担。

Result: 1. 许多现有分歧度量（如排名方差、分裂性）位于层级3，即成对比较不足以表达它们。2. 理论和实验均证明超越层级3的价值。3. 两种启发式协议在不同场景下各有优势。

Conclusion: 成对比较不足以捕捉选民偏好的结构性分歧，需要至少三元子集信息。多元矩阵提供了一种通用的、可扩展的框架来描述和计算分歧度量，并通过设计的启发式协议在实际中可行。

Abstract: We analyze the structure of the disagreement among a population of voters over a set of alternatives. Surveys typically ask either for pairwise comparisons, simple and intuitive for participants, or full rankings over alternatives, eliciting the entire voters' preferences. Building on the observation that pairwise comparisons cannot distinguish structural disagreement from noise, we propose a stratified framework to identify the minimal aggregated preference information needed to compute a number of disagreement measures from the literature. Specifically, we introduce the plurality matrix, a generalization of pairwise comparisons that records, for every subset $S$ of alternatives, the probability that each $a \in S$ ranks first in $S$. We define the level of a disagreement measure as the smallest subset size needed to express it, showing that many existing notions, including rank-variance and divisiveness, sit at level $3$, proving that pairwise comparisons are not enough. In addition, we demonstrate the interest of going beyond level $3$ both theoretically and experimentally. To make these results actionable, we design two elicitation protocols to estimate the plurality matrix, exploring the trade-off between the number of required participants and the cognitive load requested to each of them.

</details>


### [165] [Generative-Evaluative Agreement: A Necessary Validity Criterion for LLM-Enabled Adaptive Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19529)
*Grandee Lee,Yue Wang,Che Yee Lye,Luke Peh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成-评估一致性(GEA)作为衡量LLM自评自生成评估有效性的指标，发现在两阶段自适应评估中模型仅恢复约一半的预期方差(r=0.698)，且存在系统性正偏差；GEA对语法可验证技能强(r>0.7)但对设计级技能近乎为零，低技能高估会抬高路由阈值附近的分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当同一LLM同时承担生成评估项目、模拟学生回答并评分的任务时，验证循环存在自我参照问题，缺乏衡量评分函数是否忠实恢复生成函数所预设技能水平的有效性标准。

Method: 提出GEA效度标准，通过两阶段自适应评估实验直接测量LLM评分函数恢复其生成函数预设技能水平的程度，并分析不同技能类型下的GEA表现。

Result: 模型恢复约一半预期方差(r=0.698)，存在系统性正偏差；语法可验证技能GEA强(r>0.7)，设计级技能GEA近乎为零；低技能高估导致路由阈值附近分数膨胀。

Conclusion: 粒度化的、按技能分解的评分标准是增强GEA的主要机制，并提出了补充性缓解措施。

Abstract: When the same LLM generates assessment items, simulates student responses, and scores them, the validation loop is self-referential. We introduce Generative-Evaluative Agreement (GEA), a validity criterion measuring whether an LLM's scoring function recovers the skill levels its generative function was instructed to produce. In the first direct measurement of GEA on a two-stage adaptive assessment, the model recovers roughly half the intended variance r = 0.698 with systematic positive bias. GEA is strong r > 0.7 for syntactically verifiable skills but near zero for design-level skills, and low-skill overestimation inflates scores near the routing threshold. We argue that granular, skill-decomposed rubrics are the principal proposed mechanism for strengthening GEA and outline complementary mitigations.

</details>


### [166] [Library Drift: Diagnosing and Fixing a Silent Failure Mode in Self-Evolving LLM Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19576)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自进化技能库中的“库漂移”问题——无结果驱动的生命周期管理导致技能无限积累、检索退化、错误注入和性能停滞。作者提供了可复现的触发机制、追踪级诊断工具以及验证有效的治理方案（结果驱动淘汰+有限活跃容量+元技能创作先验），将MBPP+ hard-100的pass@1从0.258提升至0.584（+0.328），并通过8组消融实验剖析了各治理机制的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化技能库存在未被识别的静默故障模式——库漂移，即技能无限积累但缺乏结果驱动的生命周期管理，导致检索退化、假阳性注入和性能停滞。尽管已有现象证据（SkillsBench显示LLM创作技能增益+0.0pp，人工创作技能增益+16.2pp），但底层机制尚未被隔离和分析。

Method: (1) 可复现触发机制：通过消融实验隔离漂移——禁用技能注入（平缓底线，+0.002）和强制提前淘汰（主动伤害，-0.019）；(2) 追踪级诊断：追加式证据日志，包含每技能贡献分数、归因判定和路由器参与度指标，在影响端任务分数前暴露故障；(3) 验证修复方案：最小治理配方（结果驱动淘汰+有限活跃容量+元技能创作先验），通过8组消融实验分解各治理机制的承重作用。

Result: 在MBPP+ hard-100上经过100轮迭代，治理方案将held-out pass@1从0.258的基线提升至后期窗口均值0.584（滚动增益+0.328）。8组消融实验明确了各治理机制的负载贡献与可替代性。

Conclusion: 库漂移是自进化系统中可诊断、可修复的故障模式；本文提供了诊断工具和治理方案，为任何自进化智能体的库漂移诊断提供了具体操作手册。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries face a silent failure mode we term \emph{library drift}: unbounded skill accumulation without outcome-driven lifecycle management causes retrieval degradation, false-positive injections, and performance stagnation. Recent evaluation confirms the symptom--LLM-authored skills deliver +0.0pp gain while human-curated ones deliver +16.2pp (SkillsBench)--yet the underlying mechanism has not been isolated. We provide (1) a reproducible trigger: ablations that isolate drift--one disables skill injection (flat floor, +0.002), one imposes premature retirement (active harm, $-$0.019); (2) trace-level diagnostics: an append-only evidence log with per-skill contribution scores, attribution verdicts, and router engagement metrics that make the failure visible before it reaches end-task scores; and (3) a verified fix: a minimal governance recipe (outcome-driven retirement + bounded active-cap + meta-skill authoring prior) that lifts held-out pass@1 from a 0.258 baseline to a late-window mean of 0.584 (rolling gain $+$0.328) on MBPP+ hard-100 over 100 rounds. Eight ablations decompose which governance mechanisms are load-bearing and which are subsumed, providing a concrete playbook for diagnosing library drift in any self-evolving agent.

</details>


### [167] [SceneCode: Executable World Programs for Editable Indoor Scenes with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19587)
*Puyi Wang,Yuhao Wang,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Yangguang Li,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SceneCode是一个将自然语言提示编译为可执行代码驱动室内世界的框架，通过程序化世界生成方法，生成具有物理交互性和关节结构的场景资产，支持机器人仿真和操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内场景合成方法将生成内容表示为静态网格，仅从精选资产库继承关节结构，限制了物体级别的可控性，无法按需生成新的可交互资产。

Method: 提出SceneCode框架：1) 房间级智能体骨干将提示转为结构化房屋布局，通过规划器-设计器-评论器循环生成资产请求；2) 五种代码生成策略将请求转为部分级Blender Python程序；3) 执行引导的修复-优化循环验证程序；4) 编译为仿真就绪资产并导出SDF；5) 持久化场景状态注册表链接各组件。

Result: 实验表明，可执行世界程序提升了忠于提示的室内场景生成效果，生成的资产具有更干净的网格结构及可加载的关节元数据，在场景级合成、物体级资产质量、人工评估和下游机器人交互任务上均表现优异。

Conclusion: SceneCode通过程序化世界生成范式，将场景组装转变为可追踪、可局部编辑的世界构建过程，有效弥合了静态场景合成与物理交互式仿真之间的差距。

Abstract: Indoor scene synthesis underpins embodied AI, robotic manipulation, and simulation-based policy evaluation, where a useful scene must specify not only what the environment looks like, but also how its objects are structured. Existing pipelines, however, typically represent generated content as static meshes and inherit articulation only from curated asset libraries, which limits object-level controllability and prevents new interactable assets from being produced on demand. We address this gap by formulating physically interactable indoor scene synthesis as programmatic world generation, and present SceneCode, a framework that compiles a natural language prompt into an executable, code-driven indoor world rather than a collection of opaque meshes. A room-level agentic backbone first turns the prompt into a structured house layout and emits per-object AssetRequests through a planner--designer--critic loop. Each request is then routed to one of five code-generation strategies and converted into a synthesized part-wise Blender Python programs that are validated through an execution-guided repair-and-refine loop. The resulting programs are compiled into simulation-ready assets, and exported as SDF for physics simulation. A persistent scene-state registry links object requests, executable programs, rendered geometry, and simulation assets, turning scene assembly into a traceable and locally editable world-building process. We evaluate SceneCode across scene-level synthesis, object-level asset quality, human judgment, and downstream robot interaction. Results show that executable world programs improve prompt-faithful indoor scene generation and produce assets with cleaner mesh structure, and simulator-loadable articulation metadata. Project page: https://scene-code.github.io/.

</details>


### [168] [Towards Multi-Model LLM Schedulers: Empirical Insights into Offloading and Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19593)
*Mert Yildiz,Pietro Spadaccino,Alexey Rolich,Francesca Cuomo,Andrea Baiocchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了多模型、异构硬件环境下LLM服务中的层卸载和抢占性能影响，发现卸载导致非线性且模型依赖的解码吞吐下降，小模型对GPU驻留减少更敏感；抢占开销主要由模型状态重载主导且因模型和硬件而异；据此提出了未来调度器需考虑的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务系统主要针对单一模型优化吞吐量，但在多模型、异构硬件的场景下，资源分配、调度和抢占等问题缺乏研究，特别是GPU内存受限时需进行CPU-GPU部分卸载和抢占的情况。

Method: 对不同LLM在多种硬件平台上进行实证研究，重点分析层卸载和抢占对性能的影响，测量解码吞吐量退化、抢占开销组成及其与模型、硬件、序列长度的关系。

Result: 卸载导致强非线性和模型依赖的解码吞吐下降，小模型对GPU驻留减少更敏感；抢占开销主要由模型状态重载主导而非KV缓存传输，且在不同模型和硬件平台间差异显著；序列长度和互联带宽放大了数据移动和执行低效。

Conclusion: 未来调度器需考虑模型特定的卸载敏感性、工作负载特征以及抢占和数据传输的成本结构，以高效管理异构多模型工作负载下的混合CPU-GPU执行。

Abstract: Modern deployments of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require serving multiple models with diverse architectures, sizes, and specialization on shared, heterogeneous hardware. This setting introduces new challenges for resource allocation, dispatching, and scheduling, particularly under GPU memory constraints where partial CPU-GPU offloading and preemption become necessary. While existing systems primarily optimize throughput for a single model, comparatively little work addresses multi-model scheduling under these conditions. In this paper, we present an empirical study of how different LLMs behave across hardware platforms, focusing on the performance implications of layer offloading and preemption. We show that offloading leads to strongly non-linear and model-dependent degradation in decode throughput, with smaller models exhibiting sharper sensitivity to reduced GPU residency. We further demonstrate that preemption incurs substantial overhead, largely dominated by model state reload rather than key-value cache transfer, and that this cost varies significantly across models and hardware platforms. Additionally, we highlight the role of sequence length and interconnect bandwidth in amplifying data movement and execution inefficiencies. Based on these findings, we identify a set of key features that future schedulers must consider, including model-specific offloading sensitivity, workload characteristics, and the cost structure of preemption and data transfer. These insights provide guidance for the design of next-generation LLM serving systems capable of efficiently managing heterogeneous, multi-model workloads with hybrid CPU-GPU execution.

</details>


### [169] [EMO-BOOST: Emotion-Augmented Audio-Visual Features for Improved Generalization in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19630)
*Aritra Marik,Marcel Klemt,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Emo-Boost，一种利用情感作为高层语义线索的多模态深度伪造检测框架。通过融合基于RGB和音频的低级检测器与情感检测器EmoForensics，在FakeAVCeleb数据集上实现跨操作泛化AUC提升2.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的不断进步使伪造检测面临压力，新生成技术持续涌现，导致无法为每种操作收集训练数据。因此，泛化到训练中未见过的深度伪造类型是当前检测研究的主要挑战。

Method: 提出Emo-Boost多模态深度伪造检测框架，融合三个组件：(1) 现成的RGB聚焦检测器；(2) 现成的声学聚焦检测器；(3) 基于情感的检测器EmoForensics。EmoForensics利用视觉和音频情感识别模块，建模音视频流中情感表征的模态内和跨模态时间一致性。

Result: EmoForensics与低级聚焦方法捕捉到互补信号，二者结合后，EmoBoost在FakeAVCeleb数据集上的平均跨操作泛化AUC提升2.1%。

Conclusion: 高层语义线索（如情感）能够支持低级聚焦方法在泛化到未见操作类型方面取得更好效果，多模态融合策略有效提升了深度伪造检测的泛化能力。

Abstract: With every advancement in generative AI models, forensics is under increasing pressure. The constant emergence of new generation techniques makes it impossible to collect data for each manipulation to train a deepfake detection model. Thus, generalizing to deepfakes unseen during training is one of the major challenges in current deepfake detection research. To tackle this challenge, we employ high-level semantic cues and argue that these cues can support low-level focused approaches in generalizing to unseen types of manipulations. In this work, we study emotions as a high-level semantic cue. We propose Emo-Boost, a multimodal deepfake detection framework that fuses an off-the-shelf RGB- and acoustic-focused deepfake detector with our emotion-based deepfake detector EmoForensics. EmoForensics utilises vision and audio emotion recognition modules and models intra- and inter-modal temporal consistency in emotion representations from an audio-visual stream. We found that EmoForensics and the low-level focused method capture complementary signals. Consequently, combining both signals in EmoBoost enhances the average cross-manipulation generalization AUC by 2.1% on FakeAVCeleb.

</details>


### [170] [Pseudocode-Guided Structured Reasoning for Automating Reliable Inference in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19663)
*Weicong Ni,Tianbao Jiang,Linlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PStar框架，通过伪代码引导的结构化推理和难度特征向量，自适应选择推理路径以减少VLM幻觉，在POPE和MMStar上取得87.1%和68.0%的SOTA性能，超越GPT-4V。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在机器人自动化决策中易产生幻觉，导致安全风险，且开放世界任务难度多变，现有模型缺乏鲁棒的推理策略。

Method: 设计抽象推理函数库构建结构化伪代码策略，并提出难度特征向量(DFV)评估问题复杂度，自适应选择推理路径。

Result: POPE得分87.1%，MMStar得分68.0%，均达到SOTA，超越GPT-4V，显著降低幻觉率。

Conclusion: PStar为部署可信赖、确定性的VLM提供了验证机制，是减少视觉-语言错误的关键一步。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are becoming the cornerstone of high-level reasoning for robotic automation, enabling robots to parse natural language commands and perceive their environments. However, their susceptibility to hallucinations introduces critical failures in decision-making, posing significant safety and reliability risks in physical deployments. This challenge is exacerbated by the open-ended nature of real-world tasks, where questions vary vastly in difficulty and modality, demanding robust and adaptable reasoning strategies. To tackle this, we propose the Pseudocode-guided Structured Reasoning framework (PStar), which adaptively selects structured pseudocode reasoning paths to help VLMs perform flexible and step-by-step reasoning. We first design a set of abstract reasoning functions and formulate a structured pseudocode library to represent modular reasoning strategies. Crucially, we design a Difficulty Feature Vector (DFV) that allows the model to assess question complexity and adaptively choose appropriate reasoning strategies-enhancing robustness and interpretability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PStar significantly reduces hallucination rates, achieving state-of-the-art scores of 87.1% on POPE and 68.0% on MMStar, outperforming even GPT-4V. By providing a validated mechanism to reduce visual-language errors, PStar offers a critical step toward deploying more trustworthy and deterministic VLMs for real-world automated systems, where such errors can lead to catastrophic outcomes.

</details>


### [171] [Transforming Constraint Programs to Input for Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19671)
*Jo Devriendt,Patrick De Causmaecker,Marc Denecker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从约束优化问题的对称性自动生成局部搜索邻域的方法，并在IDP系统中实现，通过六个经典问题验证了该技术的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，将约束优化问题应用于局部搜索算法需要人工干预来编译元启发式算法的输入数据，这一过程耗时且易错，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 建立了约束优化问题的对称性与局部搜索邻域之间的联系，并利用这种联系在IDP系统中从约束规范自动生成邻域。

Result: 在六个经典优化问题上评估了自动生成的邻域，实验观察支持该技术的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于对称性自动生成局部搜索邻域是一种可行的技术，能够减少人工干预，为约束优化问题的自动化求解提供了新路径。

Abstract: Applying local search algorithms to combinatorial optimization problems is not an easy feat. Typically, human intervention is required to compile the constraints to input data for some metaheuristic algorithm. In this paper, we establish a link between symmetry properties of constraint optimization problems and local search neighborhoods, and we use this link to automatically generate neighborhoods from a constraint specification in the context of the IDP system. We evaluate the obtained neighborhoods for six classical optimization problems. The resulting observations support the viability of this technique.

</details>


### [172] [Memory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agent for CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19748)
*Yin Xiaolong,Liu Yu,Shen Jiahang,Lu Xingyu,Ni Jingzhe,Fan Fengxiao,Sang Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种记忆增强的强化学习框架用于CAD生成智能体，通过封装几何内核、构建闭环机制、双轨记忆模块和动态效用检索算法，有效解决了LLM在复杂CAD模型生成中推理链断裂和缺乏纠错机制的问题，显著提升了成功率和几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的CAD生成方法在处理具有长操作序列、多样操作类型和强几何约束的复杂CAD模型时表现不佳，主要原因是推理链容易断裂且缺乏有效的纠错机制。

Method: 1) 将底层几何内核封装为智能体可调用的结构化工具链；2) 构建了设计意图理解、全局规划、执行和多维度验证的闭环机制；3) 设计了包含案例库和技能库的双轨记忆模块，并提出动态效用检索算法；4) 引入强化学习进行检索和策略优化，使智能体能够避免语义相似但几何不可行的检索陷阱，实现在线自我纠正和持续进化。

Result: 在复杂CAD模型生成任务上，所提方法显著提升了成功率和几何一致性。

Conclusion: 通过记忆增强的强化学习框架，智能体能够在不依赖额外大规模标注数据的情况下实现在线自我纠正和持续进化，有效解决了复杂CAD模型生成的难题。

Abstract: Automatic generation of computer-aided design (CAD) models is a core technology for enabling intelligence in advanced manufacturing. Existing generation methods based on large language models (LLMs) often fall short when handling complex CAD models characterized by long operation sequences, diverse operation types, and strong geometric constraints, primarily because reasoning chains break and effective error-correction mechanisms are lacking. To address this problem, this paper proposes a memory-augmented reinforcement learning framework for CAD generation agents. The framework encapsulates the underlying geometric kernel into a structured toolchain callable by the agent and builds a closed-loop mechanism of design intent understanding, global planning, execution, and multi-dimensional verification. It also designs a dual-track memory module consisting of a case library and a skill library, and proposes a dynamic utility retrieval algorithm. By introducing reinforcement learning into retrieval and policy optimization, the agent can effectively avoid retrieval traps in which examples are semantically similar but geometrically infeasible, enabling online self-correction and continual evolution without additional large-scale annotated data. Experiments show that the proposed method significantly improves both the success rate and geometric consistency on complex CAD model generation tasks.

</details>


### [173] [CogScale: Scalable Benchmark for Sequence Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19758)
*Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Romain de Coudenhove,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CogScale基准测试，包含14个可扩展的合成任务，用于评估不同架构的认知和记忆能力。在严格参数预算下测试了7种架构，发现经典RNN和ESN在基础记忆任务上表现优异，但只有注意力机制和现代状态空间模型在推理复杂度增加时仍保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估新架构处理序列信息的能力通常需要大规模数据集和模型，计算成本高昂且迭代周期缓慢，缺乏轻量级、标准化的评估框架。

Method: 提出CogScale基准测试，包含14个可扩展的合成任务，在1k、10k、100k参数预算下评估GRU、LSTM、xLSTM、ESN、Mamba、Transformer Decoder和Transformer Encoder-Decoder七种架构。

Result: 经典RNN和ESN在严格参数预算下的基础记忆任务中表现优异；注意力机制和现代状态空间模型在推理复杂度和任务难度增加时仍能维持高性能。

Conclusion: CogScale为快速验证架构创新提供了标准化、轻量级的评估框架，有效降低了大规模训练前的验证成本。

Abstract: The ability to maintain and manipulate information over time is a fundamental aspect of living beings and Artificial Intelligence. While modern models have achieved remarkable success in tasks like natural language processing, evaluating the capacity of novel architectures to process sequential information remains computationally expensive and time-consuming. Testing a new architecture often requires scaling up to massive datasets and models, leading to vast computational costs and slow iteration cycles. In this paper, we propose CogScale, a benchmark of 14 scalable synthetic tasks designed to isolate and evaluate specific cognitive and memory abilities at different parametrizable scales. By providing a standardized, lightweight framework, CogScale allows researchers to rapidly validate architectural innovations before committing to large-scale training. To establish a solid baseline, we evaluate seven distinct architectures: Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), xLSTM, Echo State Network (ESN), Mamba, Transformer Decoder, and Transformer Encoder-Decoder. These evaluations are conducted under strict parameter budgets (1k, 10k, and 100k) and across different difficulty levels and scales. Our results show that while classical RNNs and Echo State Networks excel at basic retention within strict parameter budgets, only attention mechanisms and modern state-space models consistently maintain high performance as reasoning complexity and task difficulty scale.

</details>


### [174] [What Really Improves Mathematical Reasoning: Structured Reasoning Signals Beyond Pure Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19762)
*Yuze Zhao,Junpeng Fang,Lu Yu,Zhenya Huang,Kai Zhang,Qing Cui,Qi Liu,Jun Zhou,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在10T词元细粒度分离语料库上的受控预训练实验，发现代码本身并非通用推理增强器，而跨域结构化推理轨迹（如代码-文本和数学-文本混合）才是推理提升的真正来源；增加数学域样本密度可在不显著损失编程性能的前提下大幅提升复杂数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码已成为现代基础语言模型训练的标准组件，但其除编程之外的作用尚不明确。本文旨在通过受控实验重新审视"代码提升推理能力"这一广泛主张，厘清代码在跨域能力迁移中的真实角色。

Method: 在10T词元的细粒度域分离语料库上进行受控预训练实验，采用三组对比设计：1) 限制代码为独立可执行程序并控制Code-NL数据；2) 对比可执行代码与跨域结构化推理轨迹（代码-文本和数学-文本混合）；3) 在固定数学预算内提高结构化数学域样本密度。同时使用专家激活模式进行机制级路由分析。

Result: 1) 代码显著提升编程能力，但并非通用推理增强器，反而与知识密集型任务（尤其是复杂数学推理）存在竞争关系；2) 推理提升应归因于跨域结构化推理轨迹而非可执行代码本身；3) 提高数学域样本密度可在保持编程性能的同时大幅提升复杂数学推理；4) 数据组成效应反映在专家激活模式中，提供了跨域竞争与协同交互的机制级证据。

Conclusion: 代码并非通用推理增强器，跨域结构化推理轨迹才是推理提升的关键；数据组成效应可通过专家激活模式解释；研究结果为更精确的数据中心优化策略提供了指导。

Abstract: Code has become a standard component of modern foundation language model (LM) training, yet its role beyond programming remains unclear. We revisit the claim that code improves reasoning through controlled pretraining experiments on a 10T-token corpus with fine-grained domain separation. Our findings are threefold. First, when code is restricted to standalone executable programs and Code-NL data are controlled for, code substantially improves programming ability but does not act as a general reasoning enhancer; instead, it competes with knowledge-intensive tasks, especially complex mathematical reasoning. Second, the reasoning gains often attributed to code are better explained by cross-domain structured reasoning traces, such as code-text and math-text mixtures, rather than by executable code alone. Third, increasing the density of structured math-domain samples within a fixed math budget yields substantial gains on difficult mathematical reasoning while largely preserving programming performance, suggesting that cognitive scaffolds offer a targeted way to mitigate cross-domain trade-offs. Finally, routing analyses show that data-composition effects are reflected in expert-activation patterns, providing mechanism-level evidence for competitive and synergistic interactions across domains. Our results clarify which data characteristics transfer across capability dimensions and point to more precise data-centric optimization strategies.

</details>


### [175] [GroupAffect-4: A Multimodal Dataset of Four-Person Collaborative Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19765)
*Meisam Jamshidi Seikavandi,Alice Modica,Anna Obara,Shan Ahmed Shaffi,Fabricio Batista Narcizo,Tanya Ignatenko,Ted Vucurevich,Karim Haddad,Daniel Barratt,Daniel Overholt,Jesper Bunsow Boldt,Paolo Burelli,Andrew Burke Dittberner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GroupAffect-4是一个包含40名参与者（10组四人群组）的多模态语料库，记录他们在四种协作任务中的生理、眼动、音频、自我报告和人格数据，支持个体、人际和群体层面的情感分析，并提供了15个可基准测试的目标及基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感计算、社会信号处理和会议语料库虽然捕捉了人类互动的重要部分，但很少支持将共处群体中的情感分析作为个体、人际和群体层面的耦合过程来研究，所需信号通常分散在不同的数据集传统中。

Method: 采集40名参与者组成10个四人小组，完成信息汇集、谈判、创意生成和公共物品博弈四种生态化协作任务；每位参与者佩戴手腕生理传感器、眼动追踪眼镜和近讲麦克风，收集连续情感自评、任务后问卷、任务结果和大五人格分数，所有数据通过共享时钟对齐。

Result: 数据集覆盖超过91%的预期生理数据窗口和98%的眼动追踪窗口；谈判模块的情感操纵检验证实了任务效度；定义了涵盖个体内状态、人际间特质和群体动力学三个层面的15个可基准测试目标，并报告了留一组的可行性基线结果。

Conclusion: GroupAffect-4以BIDS启发式结构、Croissant元数据、数据表、每会话质量报告和开源处理脚本发布，为共处群体的多层面情感分析提供了全面的基准数据集。

Abstract: Existing affective-computing, social-signal-processing, and meeting corpora capture important parts of human interaction, but they rarely support analysis of affect in co-located groups as a coupled individual, interpersonal, and group-level process. The required signals (per-participant physiology, eye movement, audio, self-report, task outcomes, and personality) are usually fragmented across separate dataset traditions. We introduce GroupAffect-4, a multimodal corpus of 40 participants in 10 four-person groups, each completing four ecologically varied collaborative tasks spanning information pooling, negotiation, idea generation, and a public-goods game. Each participant is instrumented with a wrist-worn physiology sensor, eye-tracking glasses, and a close-talk microphone; sessions include continuous affect self-reports, post-task questionnaires, task outcomes, and Big-Five personality scores, all time-aligned to a shared clock. The dataset covers over 91% of expected physiology windows and 98% of eye-tracking windows, with strong task validity confirmed by a clear affective manipulation check across the negotiation block. We define fifteen benchmarkable targets spanning three analysis levels -- within-person state, between-person traits, and group dynamics -- and report leave-one-group-out feasibility baselines establishing the dataset's evaluative scope. GroupAffect-4 is released with a BIDS-inspired structure, Croissant metadata, a datasheet, per-session quality reports, and open processing scripts. Code and processing scripts are available at https://github.com/meisamjam/GroupAffect-4; the dataset is publicly archived at https://zenodo.org/records/20037847.

</details>


### [176] [Minimax Optimal Variance-Aware Regret Bounds for Multinomial Logistic MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19768)
*Pierre Boudart,Pierre Gaillard,Alessandro Rudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对基于多项逻辑(MNL)模型的混合马尔可夫决策过程(MDPs)，提出了一个引入问题依赖常数σ̄_T的强化学习算法，实现了Õ(dH²σ̄_T√T)的遗憾界，在结构化MDP上优于现有结果，并给出了匹配的下界证明，首次完整刻画了MNL混合MDP的遗憾复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MNL混合MDP算法（Li et al., 2024）的遗憾界为Õ(dH²√T)，忽略了问题内在结构带来的潜在改进空间。受逻辑bandit文献启发，作者希望引入问题依赖的方差度量，以更精细地表征MNL混合MDP的遗憾复杂度，并在结构化MDP（如KL约束鲁棒MDP）中获得更好的理论保证。

Method: 引入问题依赖常数σ̄_T ≤ 1/2，度量最优下游值函数沿着学习者轨迹的归一化平均方差。基于此设计新算法，在遗憾界中实现σ̄_T因子的改进。

Result: 提出算法达到Õ(dH²σ̄_T√T)的遗憾界，在worst-case下恢复现有界，在结构化MDP中显著改进（如KL约束鲁棒MDP中σ̄_T=O(H⁻¹)，减少了H因子）。同时建立了匹配的Ω(dH²σ̄_T√T)下界，证明算法在log因子意义下是最小化最优的。

Conclusion: 首次完整刻画了MNL混合MDP的遗憾复杂度，证明了问题的本质困难由特征维度d、回合长度H和问题依赖方差σ̄_T共同决定。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning for episodic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) whose transitions are modelled by a multinomial logistic (MNL) model. Existing algorithms for MNL mixture MDPs yield a regret of $\smash{\tilde{O}(dH^2\sqrt{T})}$ (Li et al., 2024), where $d$ is the feature dimension, $H$ the episode length, and $T$ the number of episodes. Inspired by the logistic bandit literature (Abeille et al., 2021; Faury et al., 2022; Boudart et al., 2026), we introduce a problem-dependent constant $\barσ\_T \leq 1/2$, measuring the normalised average variance of the optimal downstream value function along the learner's trajectory. We propose an algorithm achieving a regret of $\smash{\tilde{O}(dH^2\barσ\_T\sqrt{T})}$, which recovers the existing bound in the worst case and improves upon it for structured MDPs. For instance, for KL-constrained robust MDPs, $\barσ\_T = O(H^{-1})$, reducing the horizon dependence by a factor $H$. We further establish a matching $\smash{Ω(dH^2\barσ\_T\sqrt{T})}$ lower bound, proving minimax optimality (up to logarithmic factors) and fully characterising the regret complexity of MNL mixture MDPs for the first time.

</details>


### [177] [Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification for Continuous AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19779)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将分裂共形预测和自适应共形推断（ACI）应用于连续AI智能体评估，在无需分布假设的情况下为预测质量分数提供覆盖保证。在24小时预测窗口内，共形区间在所有名义水平上的标定误差低于0.02；ACI在智能体版本更新后正确地将区间宽度扩大35%并重新收敛。进一步开发了多智能体流水线的组合不确定性界、成对排序的共形弃权规则以及排行榜级多重检验的FDR校正弃权。基于50个智能体和18个实时信号（每小时采集）的实验表明，每个智能体的条件覆盖率高度集中在名义水平附近（均值80.4%，90%智能体落在[72%, 90%]），跨来源情感分歧可预测排名不稳定性（r=0.64, p<0.01），循环控制验证确认框架捕捉到了超越基准的信号（rho_s=0.52, p<0.01, n=35）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将统计共形预测方法引入连续AI智能体评估场景，解决实时质量评分缺乏分布自由覆盖保证的问题，并应对智能体版本更新带来的分布偏移挑战。

Method: 1) 采用分裂共形预测（split conformal prediction）和自适应共形推断（ACI）构建预测区间；2) 提出多智能体流水线的组合不确定性界，通过模拟验证跨阶段相关性rho∈[-0.5, 0.9]下的有效性；3) 设计共形弃权规则控制成对排序的错误排名率，并推广为FDR校正弃权用于排行榜多假设检验；4) 基于50个智能体和18个每小时采集的实时信号进行实证评估。

Result: 共形区间在24小时窗口标定误差<0.02；ACI在智能体发布后正确扩大区间35%并收敛；每个智能体条件覆盖率均值80.4%，90%智能体在[72%,90%]内；跨来源情感分歧与排名不稳定性显著相关（r=0.64, p<0.01）；循环控制验证确认框架捕捉超越基准的信号（rho_s=0.52, p<0.01）。

Conclusion: 所提出的共形评估框架为连续AI智能体监控提供了分布自由的理论保证和可靠的实践经验，代码和数据以CC BY 4.0协议开源。

Abstract: We adapt split conformal prediction and adaptive conformal inference (ACI) to continuous AI agent evaluation, providing distribution-free coverage guarantees for forecasted quality scores. Conformal intervals achieve calibration error below 0.02 across all nominal levels at the 24h horizon, while ACI correctly widens intervals by 35% following agent releases then reconverges. We further develop compositional uncertainty bounds for multi-agent pipelines (validated via simulation across inter-stage correlations rho in [-0.5, 0.9]), a conformal abstention rule for pairwise rankings with controlled false-ranking rate, and FDR-corrected abstention for leaderboard-scale multiple testing. Evaluating 50 agents via 18 real-time signals collected hourly, we show that per-agent conditional coverage is well-concentrated around the nominal level (mean 80.4%, 90% of agents within [72%, 90%]), and that cross-source sentiment divergence predicts ranking instability (r=0.64, p<0.01). A circularity-controlled validation confirms the framework captures signal beyond benchmarks (rho_s=0.52, p<0.01, n=35). Code and data are released under CC BY 4.0.

</details>


### [178] [From Prompts to Pavement Through Time: Temporal Grounding in Agentic Scene-to-Plan Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19824)
*Ahmed Y. Gado,Omar Y. Goba,Alaa Hassanein,Catherine M. Elias,Ahmed Hussein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶中多智能体大模型通信的时间条件作用，发现虽然时间条件能重塑推理风格（如预测性危险推理、稳定纠错行为），但在标准NLP指标上无显著提升，建立了首个时序场景到规划推理的实证基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶中利用LLM/LMM进行高层场景理解与规划的方法将时间视为次要属性，缺乏时间基础导致连续动作推理不一致，影响安全性和可解释性。因此探索智能体间通信中的时间条件作用能否在不牺牲语义和逻辑一致性的前提下增强推理连贯性。

Method: 设计了三种时间集成程度递增的规划器架构，在BDD-X数据集的精选子集上使用语义、句法和逻辑指标进行评估，并进行定性分析。

Result: 时间条件作用在标准NLP正确性指标上无统计学显著提升，但定性分析显示出预测性危险推理、稳定纠错行为和Sentinel中的策略性分歧。

Conclusion: 揭示了基于提示的时间基础能力的局限性，建立了首个时序场景到规划推理的实证基准。

Abstract: Recent attempts to support high-level scene interpretation and planning in Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) using ensembles of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) continue to treat time as a secondary property. This lack of temporal grounding leads to inconsistencies in reasoning about continuous actions, undermining both safety and interpretability. This work explores whether temporal conditioning within inter-agent communication can preserve or enhance coherence without introducing degradation in semantic or logical consistency. To investigate this, we introduce three planner architectures with progressively increasing temporal integration and evaluate them on curated subsets of the BDD-X dataset using semantic, syntactic, and logical metrics. Results show that while temporal conditioning reshapes reasoning style, it yields no statistically significant improvements in standard NLP-based correctness metrics. However, qualitative analysis reveals predictive hazard reasoning, stable corrective behavior, and strategic divergence in the Sentinel. These findings clarify the limits of prompt-based temporal grounding and establish the first empirical benchmark for temporal scene-to-plan reasoning.

</details>


### [179] [Streamlined Constraint Reasoning via CNN Pattern Recognition on Enumerated Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19895)
*Patrick Spracklen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合对比训练CNN与LLM的新型Streamliner自动合成方法，通过枚举可行解训练CNN检测结构模式，再引导LLM生成约束。在三个基准问题上实现89-99%的求解时间缩减，加速比达356-1103x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Streamliner自动合成方法或搜索约束语法库，或直接基于问题模型提示LLM，未利用可行解的结构信息。本文旨在通过CNN从解空间提取结构模式，再引导LLM生成更精准的Streamliner约束。

Method: 枚举可行解构造正样本，对解进行扰动生成负样本，对比训练CNN区分解与非解；将CNN的判别信号（如激活模式）作为上下文输入LLM，引导其生成MiniZinc格式的Streamliner候选约束；在已应用标准硬化的基准模型上评估。

Result: Vessel Loading上求解时间减少98.8%，Social Golfers减少98.6%，Black Hole减少89.4%；最佳单一Streamliner的几何加速比分别为932x、356x、1103x；发现的约束包括Vessel Loading的类级装箱约束、Social Golfers的超硬化规范约束、Black Hole的布局坐标边界。

Conclusion: CNN引导的LLM Streamliner合成方法优于纯模型文本提示方法，有效利用解的结构信息生成高质量约束，在硬化基准上取得显著加速效果。

Abstract: Constraint programming practitioners accelerate hard problems through a layered set of techniques applied in order of risk. Standard hardening (symmetry-breaking and implied constraints) is applied first and preserves satisfiability. Streamliner constraints, which restrict search to a structural sub-family of solutions, do not preserve satisfiability and are reserved as a final lever. Existing automated streamliner-synthesis approaches either search a constraint grammar or prompt a Large Language Model directly on the problem model. We propose a different approach: enumerate feasible solutions, train a Convolutional Neural Network contrastively against perturbed non-solutions to detect structural patterns, and translate the CNN's discriminative signal into candidate MiniZinc streamliners through LLM-driven synthesis. The CNN grounds the LLM's constraint generation in observed solution structure rather than model text alone. We evaluate on hardened benchmark models where streamliner discovery is the residual performance lever. Our pipeline achieves 98.8% portfolio time reduction on hardened Vessel Loading, 98.6% on hardened Social Golfers, and 89.4% on Black Hole, with best-single streamliners reaching geometric-mean speedups of 932x, 356x, and 1103x respectively. Discovered streamliners include class-based packing constraints on Vessel Loading, beyond-hardening canonicalisations on Social Golfers, and layout-coordinate bounds on Black Hole.

</details>


### [180] [Robotics-Inspired Guardrails for Foundation Models in Socially Sensitive Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19940)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Drazen Brscic,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将基础模型的护栏问题重新定义为交互轨迹的运行时行为控制问题，借鉴机器人学中的形式化约束方法，并构建了Grounded Observer框架，在闲聊、自闭症家庭治疗和学校行为降级三个真实场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏方法（训练时对齐、提示工程、解码约束、事后审核）主要提供经验性风险降低而非可执行的行为保证，且将安全性视为单个输出的属性而非交互轨迹的属性。在基础教育、心理健康等敏感领域，失败往往是累积性和上下文依赖的，亟需更强的行为保证。

Method: 借鉴机器人学中不确定闭环系统的约束强制执行方法，将护栏重构为对交互轨迹的运行时行为控制问题，提出Grounded Observer框架，在三个真实部署场景中实现运行时干预，在适应多样化社会上下文的同时防止交互漂移到不良状态。

Result: 在闲聊、自闭症家庭治疗和学校行为降级三个场景中，该框架能够实现运行时干预，有效缓解交互向不良状态的漂移，同时适应不同的社会情境。

Conclusion: 将护栏问题形式化为交互轨迹的运行时控制是可取的路径，讨论框架扩展并提出向更强保证的研究方向。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed in socially sensitive domains such as education, mental health, and caregiving, where failures are often cumulative and context-dependent. Existing guardrail approaches -- ranging from training-time alignment to prompting, decoding constraints, and post-hoc moderation -- primarily provide empirical risk reduction rather than enforceable behavioral guarantees, and largely treat safety as a property of individual outputs rather than interaction trajectories. We reframe guardrails as a problem of runtime behavioral control over interaction trajectories, drawing on robotics to introduce formal constructs for constraint enforcement in uncertain, closed-loop systems. We instantiate these ideas in the Grounded Observer framework and apply it across three real-world deployments: small talk, in-home autism therapy, and behavioral de-escalation in schools. Across settings, the framework enables runtime interventions that mitigate drift into undesirable interaction regimes while adapting to diverse social contexts. We discuss extensions to the framework and propose research directions toward stronger guarantees.

</details>


### [181] [Probabilistic Tiny Recursive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19943)
*Amin Sghaier,Ali Parviz,Alexia Jolicoeur-Martineau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PTRM通过在TRM的递归步骤中注入高斯噪声实现随机探索，以任务无关的方式扩展测试时计算，在多个推理基准上以极少的参数（7M）大幅超越LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRM的确定性递归易收敛于次优解且缺乏逃逸机制，现有解决方案依赖任务特定的测试时输入扰动和投票聚合，缺乏通用性。

Method: 在TRM每次深度递归步骤中注入高斯噪声，生成多条并行探索轨迹，利用模型原有的Q头（原用于早停）在轨迹间进行选择，无需重新训练或任务特定增强。

Result: Sudoku-Extreme准确率从87.4%提升至98.75%；Pencil Puzzle Bench从62.6%提升至91.2%，是前沿LLM的1.66倍（91.2% vs 55.1%），成本仅为0.0001倍，仅用7M参数。
